```sql
-- test: nobody records a door as someone else, for another campaign, or changes one
begin;
set local role authenticated;
set local request.jwt.claim.sub = '00000000-0000-0000-0000-0000000000b2';
do $$
begin
  assert pg_temp.fails_with(
    $q$insert into public.house_visits (client_id, pc, outcome, visited_at, actor)
       values (gen_random_uuid(), '6GCRPQM6+2A', 'spoke', now(), '00000000-0000-0000-0000-0000000000c3')$q$,
    'new row violates row-level security policy for table "house_visits"');
  assert pg_temp.fails_with(
    $q$insert into public.house_visits (campaign_id, client_id, pc, outcome, visited_at)
       values ('ca000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000003', gen_random_uuid(), '6GCRPQM6+2A', 'spoke', now())$q$,
    'new row violates row-level security policy "own campaign only" for table "house_visits"');
  assert pg_temp.fails_with(
    $q$update public.house_visits set outcome = 'spoke'$q$,
    'permission denied for table house_visits');
end $$;
rollback;

-- test: doors per ward and per house come from each house's latest visit in 30 days
begin;
set local role authenticated;
set local request.jwt.claim.sub = '00000000-0000-0000-0000-0000000000c3';
insert into public.house_visits (client_id, pc, ward_id, outcome, support, visited_at)
select gen_random_uuid(), v.pc, w.id, v.outcome, v.support, now() - v.age
  from (values ('6GCRPQM6+2A', 'kileleshwa', 'spoke', 5, interval '40 days'),
               ('6GCRPQM6+2A', 'kileleshwa', 'not_home', null, interval '2 days'),
               ('6GCRPQM6+3B', 'kileleshwa', 'spoke', 4, interval '1 day'),
               ('6GCRPQM6+5D', 'kawangware', 'refused', null, interval '0 days')
       ) as v(pc, ward, outcome, support, age)
  join public.wards w on w.slug = v.ward and w.campaign_id = 'ca000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000002';
set local request.jwt.claim.sub = '00000000-0000-0000-0000-0000000000b2';
insert into public.house_visits (client_id, pc, ward_id, outcome, support, visited_at)
select gen_random_uuid(), '6GCRPQM6+4C', w.id, 'spoke', 2, now()
  from public.wards w
 where w.slug = 'kileleshwa' and w.campaign_id = 'ca000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000002';
set local request.jwt.claim.sub = '00000000-0000-0000-0000-0000000000c3';
do $$
declare
  kile uuid := (select id from public.wards
                 where slug = 'kileleshwa' and campaign_id = 'ca000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000002');
  d record;
begin
  select * into d from public.house_doors() where ward_id = kile;
  assert d.knocked = 3 and d.rated = 2 and d.supporters = 1, 'A, B and C; B and C scored; B a supporter';
  assert d.doors_week = 3, 'three doors this week: the visit 40 days ago is older';
  assert d.agents_today = array['00000000-0000-0000-0000-0000000000b2'::uuid], 'only C was knocked today';
  assert (select string_agg(pc || ' ' || outcome, ', ' order by pc) from public.ward_houses(kile))
         = '6GCRPQM6+2A not_home, 6GCRPQM6+3B spoke, 6GCRPQM6+4C spoke';
end $$;
set local request.jwt.claim.sub = '00000000-0000-0000-0000-0000000000b2';
do $$
declare
  kile uuid := (select id from public.wards
                 where slug = 'kileleshwa' and campaign_id = 'ca000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000002');
  kawa uuid := (select id from public.wards
                 where slug = 'kawangware' and campaign_id = 'ca000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000002');
  d record;
begin
  select * into d from public.house_doors() where ward_id = kile;
  assert d.knocked = 1 and d.rated = 1 and d.supporters = 0, 'the agent counts the door they knocked';
  assert not exists (select 1 from public.house_doors() where ward_id = kawa),
    'and not the manager''s door in Kawangware';
end $$;
rollback;

-- test: a house keeps its last score when a later visit finds no one home
begin;
set local role authenticated;
set local request.jwt.claim.sub = '00000000-0000-0000-0000-0000000000c3';
insert into public.house_visits (client_id, pc, ward_id, outcome, support, visited_at)
select gen_random_uuid(), '6GCRPQM6+6E', w.id, v.outcome, v.support, now() - v.age
  from (values ('spoke', 4, interval '10 days'),
               ('not_home', null, interval '1 day')
       ) as v(outcome, support, age)
  join public.wards w on w.slug = 'kileleshwa' and w.campaign_id = 'ca000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000002';
do $$
declare
  kile uuid := (select id from public.wards
                 where slug = 'kileleshwa' and campaign_id = 'ca000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000002');
  d record;
  h record;
begin
  select * into d from public.house_doors() where ward_id = kile;
  assert d.knocked = 1 and d.rated = 1 and d.supporters = 1,
    'one house, knocked, and still a supporter by the score it gave ten days ago';
  assert (select count(*) from public.ward_houses(kile)) = 1, 'the house once';
  select * into h from public.ward_houses(kile);
  assert h.pc = '6GCRPQM6+6E' and h.outcome = 'not_home' and h.support = 4,
    'the latest outcome, and the score from the visit before';
end $$;
rollback;

-- test: a retried sync is a repeat when only house_visits has the visit
begin;
set local role authenticated;
set local request.jwt.claim.sub = '00000000-0000-0000-0000-0000000000b2';
-- A visit on a house with no event behind it (part 3 adds doors with no person).
insert into public.house_visits (client_id, pc, outcome, visited_at)
values (gen_random_uuid(), '6GCRPQM6+7F', 'not_home', now());
do $$
declare
  visit uuid := (select client_id from public.house_visits where pc = '6GCRPQM6+7F');
  people_before bigint := (select count(*) from public.people);
  events_before bigint := (select count(*) from public.person_events);
  r jsonb;
begin
  assert not exists (select 1 from public.person_events where client_id = visit),
    'person_events has no row for this visit';
  r := public.record_door(visit, null, '{"phone": "+254722000333", "name": "Wanjiru"}',
         'spoke', 4, 'Water', '{}', null, now(),
         '{"lat": -1.26532, "lng": 36.76093, "accuracy": 8, "buildingId": "6GCRPQM6+7F"}');
  assert (r ->> 'repeat')::boolean, 'the visit on the house makes it a repeat';
  assert (select count(*) from public.people) = people_before, 'no person added';
  assert (select count(*) from public.person_events) = events_before, 'no person event added';
  assert (select count(*) from public.house_visits) = 1, 'no house visit added';
end $$;
rollback;

-- test: a visit with no ward is left out of the ward counts
begin;
set local role authenticated;
set local request.jwt.claim.sub = '00000000-0000-0000-0000-0000000000c3';
insert into public.house_visits (client_id, pc, ward_id, outcome, support, visited_at)
select gen_random_uuid(), v.pc, w.id, v.outcome, v.support, now() - v.age
  from (values ('6GCRPQM6+2A', 'kileleshwa', 'spoke', 4, interval '3 days'),
               ('6GCRPQM6+5D', 'kawangware', 'refused', null, interval '2 days')
       ) as v(pc, ward, outcome, support, age)
  join public.wards w on w.slug = v.ward and w.campaign_id = 'ca000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000002';
-- Someone with no ward on file answered at these: a house that also has a visit
-- with a ward (the newer visit changes nothing for it), and a house with none.
insert into public.house_visits (client_id, pc, outcome, support, visited_at)
values (gen_random_uuid(), '6GCRPQM6+2A', 'not_home', null, now() - interval '1 day'),
       (gen_random_uuid(), '6GCRPQM6+8G', 'spoke', 5, now() - interval '1 day');
do $$
declare
  kile uuid := (select id from public.wards
                 where slug = 'kileleshwa' and campaign_id = 'ca000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000002');
  kawa uuid := (select id from public.wards
                 where slug = 'kawangware' and campaign_id = 'ca000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000002');
  d record;
begin
  assert not exists (select 1 from public.house_doors() where ward_id is null), 'no row without a ward';
  assert (select count(*) from public.house_doors()) = 2, 'a row for each of the two wards';
  select * into d from public.house_doors() where ward_id = kile;
  assert d.knocked = 1 and d.rated = 1 and d.supporters = 1 and d.doors_week = 1,
    'Kileleshwa counts only the visit that has a ward';
  select * into d from public.house_doors() where ward_id = kawa;
  assert d.knocked = 1 and d.rated = 0 and d.supporters = 0 and d.doors_week = 1, 'Kawangware as it was';
  assert (select string_agg(pc || ' ' || outcome, ', ' order by pc) from public.ward_houses(kile))
         = '6GCRPQM6+2A spoke', 'and the house keeps the visit that has a ward';
end $$;
rollback;

-- test: visits at the same moment resolve the same way every time
begin;
set local role authenticated;
set local request.jwt.claim.sub = '00000000-0000-0000-0000-0000000000c3';
-- Three visits at each of three houses, all at the same moment. The one created
-- last wins; of two created together, the one with the higher id. The ids run
-- against the creation order and go in lowest first, so neither the order of
-- the rows nor either rule alone picks the right visit.
--   2A: not_home wins over spoke and refused (the outcome).
--   3B: the score 5 wins over 2 and 1.
--   5E: the visit in Kileleshwa wins over two in Kawangware (the ward).
insert into public.house_visits (id, client_id, pc, ward_id, outcome, support, visited_at, created_at)
select ('00000000-0000-4000-8000-0000000000' || v.n)::uuid, gen_random_uuid(), v.pc, w.id,
       v.outcome, v.support, now() - interval '2 days', now() - v.made
  from (values ('21', '6GCRPQM6+2A', 'kileleshwa', 'spoke', null, interval '60 minutes'),
               ('22', '6GCRPQM6+2A', 'kileleshwa', 'not_home', null, interval '60 minutes'),
               ('23', '6GCRPQM6+2A', 'kileleshwa', 'refused', null, interval '90 minutes'),
               ('31', '6GCRPQM6+3B', 'kileleshwa', 'spoke', 2, interval '60 minutes'),
               ('32', '6GCRPQM6+3B', 'kileleshwa', 'spoke', 5, interval '60 minutes'),
               ('33', '6GCRPQM6+3B', 'kileleshwa', 'spoke', 1, interval '90 minutes'),
               ('51', '6GCRPQM6+5E', 'kawangware', 'refused', null, interval '60 minutes'),
               ('52', '6GCRPQM6+5E', 'kileleshwa', 'refused', null, interval '60 minutes'),
               ('53', '6GCRPQM6+5E', 'kawangware', 'refused', null, interval '90 minutes')
       ) as v(n, pc, ward, outcome, support, made)
  join public.wards w on w.slug = v.ward and w.campaign_id = 'ca000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000002'
 order by v.n;
do $$
declare
  kile uuid := (select id from public.wards
                 where slug = 'kileleshwa' and campaign_id = 'ca000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000002');
  kawa uuid := (select id from public.wards
                 where slug = 'kawangware' and campaign_id = 'ca000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000002');
  d record;
begin
  select * into d from public.house_doors() where ward_id = kile;
  assert d.knocked = 3, 'all three houses are in Kileleshwa, 5E by its visit created last';
  assert d.rated = 1 and d.supporters = 1, '3B is scored 5, by the visit created last';
  assert coalesce((select knocked from public.house_doors() where ward_id = kawa), 0) = 0,
    'and none is in Kawangware';
  assert (select string_agg(pc || ' ' || outcome || ' ' || coalesce(support::text, '-'), ', ' order by pc)
            from public.ward_houses(kile))
         = '6GCRPQM6+2A not_home -, 6GCRPQM6+3B spoke 5, 6GCRPQM6+5E refused -';
end $$;
rollback;
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Run the SQL tests to see it fail**

Run: `bash tests/sql/run.sh`
Expected: `FAIL tests/sql/houses.test.sql`, with an error that column `map_area` does not exist. Every other file still says `ok`.

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the migration**

Create `supabase/migrations/20261012090000_voters_map.sql`. The `record_door` in it is the whole function from `20260924160000_map_places.sql` with exactly two changes, marked by the comments: the repeat check also looks in `house_visits`, and a visit with a house inserts a `house_visits` row after the `person_events` insert. `house_visits` has the `stamp_campaign` trigger every campaign table has (AGENTS.md), with the person and then the ward as parents.

```sql
-- Schema 23: the Voters map, part 1 (docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-12-voters-map-design.md).
--
-- 1. Each campaign's map area: the atlas key of the area its Voters map covers.
--    Only areas with a house file in public/tiles work (src/lib/map-areas.ts).
-- 2. Kalonzo's workspace takes Nairobi's 85 wards, the map area chosen for it.
-- 3. house_visits: every door knocked, counted against the house (its plus
--    code from the house file), written by record_door.
-- 4. house_doors() and ward_houses(): the doors per ward and per house, for
--    the map's colours.
-- 5. Door visits already on record with a house are copied in once.

-- 1. The map area lives on the campaign: only Groundwork sets it, because it
-- needs a house file, and atlas_settings has no row until someone picks a home.
alter table public.campaigns
  add column if not exists map_area text
  check (map_area is null or map_area ~ '^[a-z0-9-]+(/[a-z0-9-]+)*$');

update public.campaigns set map_area = 'nairobi'
 where id in ('ca000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001', 'ca000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000002');
update public.campaigns set map_area = 'nyeri/mathira'
 where id = 'ca000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000003';

-- 2. Kalonzo's wards: names and constituencies from Sakaja's rows, registered
-- voters from the 2022 register (data/atlas/nairobi/register.csv; 2,415,310 in
-- all), no targets and no supporters yet.
insert into public.wards (campaign_id, slug, name, constituency, registered_voters, target_votes, supporters)
select 'ca000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001', w.slug, w.name, w.constituency, r.registered, 0, 0
  from public.wards w
  join (values
    ('gatina', 34831), ('kabiro', 29090), ('kawangware', 28468), ('kileleshwa', 26343),
    ('kilimani', 38927), ('mutu-ini', 13484), ('ngando', 24892), ('riruta', 37766),
    ('uthiru-ruthimitu', 19657), ('waithaka', 19131), ('kayole-central', 26855),
    ('kayole-north', 25563), ('kayole-south', 41154), ('komarock', 33127),
    ('matopeni-spring-valley', 19193), ('embakasi', 46291), ('lower-savannah', 30104),
    ('mihango', 23569), ('upper-savannah', 28928), ('utawala', 25707), ('dandora-area-i', 20543),
    ('dandora-area-ii', 19761), ('dandora-area-iii', 21247), ('dandora-area-iv', 27293),
    ('kariobangi-north', 24500), ('imara-daima', 38096), ('kwa-njenga', 28554),
    ('kwa-reuben', 33591), ('kware', 29812), ('pipeline', 37900), ('kariobangi-south', 38339),
    ('mowlem', 23423), ('umoja-i', 40554), ('umoja-ii', 39562), ('airbase', 16352),
    ('california', 17492), ('eastleigh-north', 27523), ('eastleigh-south', 28019),
    ('pumwani', 39130), ('clay-city', 34387), ('kasarani', 27525), ('mwiki', 27837),
    ('njiru', 28140), ('ruai', 37361), ('laini-saba', 19378), ('lindi', 19082),
    ('makina', 28408), ('sarangombe', 32442), ('woodley-kenyatta-golf-course', 28972),
    ('karen', 34450), ('mugumo-ini', 37864), ('nairobi-west', 18386), ('nyayo-highrise', 21800),
    ('south-c', 33149), ('harambee', 36498), ('makongeni', 28658), ('maringo-hamza', 39821),
    ('viwandani', 24650), ('hospital', 12512), ('huruma', 23018), ('kiamaiko', 19193),
    ('mabatini', 20976), ('mlango-kubwa', 22765), ('ngei', 24699), ('githurai', 35899),
    ('kahawa', 17428), ('kahawa-west', 38586), ('roysambu', 31158), ('zimmerman', 30701),
    ('baba-dogo', 29419), ('korogocho', 17440), ('lucky-summer', 20690),
    ('mathare-north', 29334), ('utalii', 27599), ('landimawe', 20436),
    ('nairobi-central', 52186), ('nairobi-south', 33853), ('ngara', 27816), ('pangani', 18494),
    ('ziwani-kariokor', 16790), ('kangemi', 32551), ('karura', 29548), ('kitisuru', 29237),
    ('mountain-view', 32259), ('parklands-highridge', 37144)
  ) as r(slug, registered) on r.slug = w.slug
 where w.campaign_id = 'ca000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000002'
on conflict (campaign_id, slug) do nothing;

-- 3. Every door knocked at a house.
create table public.house_visits (
  id          uuid primary key default gen_random_uuid(),
  campaign_id uuid not null default public.my_campaign()
              references public.campaigns(id) on delete cascade,
  -- Made on the phone, so a visit synced twice is counted once.
  client_id   uuid not null,
  -- The house's plus code, as the house file names it.
  pc          text not null check (pc ~ '^[0-9A-Z+]{4,24}$'),
  ward_id     uuid references public.wards(id) on delete set null,
  person_id   uuid references public.people(id) on delete set null,
  outcome     text not null check (outcome in ('spoke', 'not_home', 'refused')),
  support     smallint check (support between 1 and 5),
  issue       text check (char_length(issue) <= 120),
  actor       uuid default auth.uid(),
  visited_at  timestamptz not null,
  lat         double precision,
  lng         double precision,
  accuracy_m  real,
  created_at  timestamptz not null default now(),
  unique (campaign_id, client_id)
);
create index house_visits_ward_idx on public.house_visits (campaign_id, ward_id, visited_at desc);
create index house_visits_pc_idx on public.house_visits (campaign_id, pc, visited_at desc);
-- person_id is on delete set null: without this, each person deleted scans every visit.
create index house_visits_person_idx on public.house_visits (person_id);
create trigger stamp_campaign before insert on public.house_visits
  for each row execute function public.stamp_campaign('people', 'person_id', 'wards', 'ward_id');

alter table public.house_visits enable row level security;
revoke all on public.house_visits from anon, authenticated;
grant select, insert on public.house_visits to authenticated;
grant all on public.house_visits to service_role;
create policy "own campaign only" on public.house_visits as restrictive for all to authenticated
  using (campaign_id = public.my_campaign()) with check (campaign_id = public.my_campaign());
-- The office reads every door; an agent the doors they knocked (part 3 adds
-- the houses on their walk lists).
-- Sub-selects, so Postgres evaluates the calls once per query, not once per row: every door is a row.
create policy "read by the office" on public.house_visits for select to authenticated
  using ((select public.my_campaign_role()) in ('super', 'candidate', 'manager', 'organiser'));
create policy "agents read their own" on public.house_visits for select to authenticated
  using (actor = (select auth.uid()));
create policy "team records their visits" on public.house_visits for insert to authenticated
  with check (public.is_team_member(auth.uid()) and actor = auth.uid());

-- record_door, as 20260924160000_map_places.sql left it, plus two changes: a
-- repeat is also a visit already on a house, and a visit with a house is
-- counted against it.
create or replace function public.record_door(
  _client_id      uuid,
  _person_id      uuid,
  _new            jsonb,
  _outcome        text,
  _support        integer,
  _issue          text,
  _consent        jsonb,
  _consent_source text,
  _visited_at     timestamptz,
  _place          jsonb default null
)
returns jsonb
language plpgsql
security invoker
set search_path = public
as $$
declare
  _pid      uuid := _person_id;
  _at       timestamptz;
  _phone    text;
  _created  boolean := false;
  _kind     text;
  _opted    boolean;
  _sms      boolean := coalesce((_consent ->> 'sms')::boolean, false);
  _wa       boolean := coalesce((_consent ->> 'whatsapp')::boolean, false);
  _call     boolean := coalesce((_consent ->> 'call')::boolean, false);
  _channels text;
  _lat      double precision;
  _lng      double precision;
  _acc      real;
  _bld      text;
begin
  if _client_id is null then
    raise exception 'Each visit needs its own id.' using errcode = 'P0001';
  end if;
  -- Two syncs of the same visit at once (open tabs share the phone's queue):
  -- the second waits here for the first, then finds the visit on record.
  perform pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtextextended(_client_id::text, 0));
  -- A retried sync: the visit is already on record.
  if exists (select 1 from public.person_events where client_id = _client_id)
     or exists (select 1 from public.house_visits where client_id = _client_id) then
    return jsonb_build_object('repeat', true);
  end if;

  if _outcome is null or _outcome not in ('spoke', 'not_home', 'refused') then
    raise exception 'Unknown outcome.' using errcode = 'P0001';
  end if;
  if _support is not null and _support not between 1 and 5 then
    raise exception 'Support is 1 to 5.' using errcode = 'P0001';
  end if;
  if (_sms or _wa or _call) and coalesce(btrim(_consent_source), '') = '' then
    raise exception 'Say how they agreed to be contacted.' using errcode = 'P0001';
  end if;

  -- The time the door was knocked, kept honest: not in the future, and not
  -- older than a week (a phone that was offline that long should say so).
  _at := least(now(), greatest(coalesce(_visited_at, now()), now() - interval '7 days'));

  -- Where the door was. A bad or vague fix is dropped, never an error.
  if jsonb_typeof(_place) = 'object' then
    if jsonb_typeof(_place -> 'lat') = 'number' and jsonb_typeof(_place -> 'lng') = 'number' then
      _lat := (_place ->> 'lat')::double precision;
      _lng := (_place ->> 'lng')::double precision;
      -- Outside Kenya is a broken fix, not a place.
      if _lat not between -5 and 5.5 or _lng not between 33.5 and 42.5 then
        _lat := null;
        _lng := null;
      end if;
    end if;
    if jsonb_typeof(_place -> 'accuracy') = 'number' then
      _acc := least(greatest((_place ->> 'accuracy')::real, 0), 100000);
      -- Wider than a few streets: too vague to pin anyone's home.
      if _acc > 250 then
        _lat := null;
        _lng := null;
      end if;
    end if;
    _bld := nullif(btrim(_place ->> 'buildingId'), '');
    if _bld is not null and _bld !~ '^[0-9A-Z+]{4,24}$' then
      _bld := null;
    end if;
  end if;

  if _pid is null then
    -- Someone met at the door. A number already on file means we know them:
    -- the visit goes on their record and only blanks are filled.
    _phone := _new ->> 'phone';
    if _phone is null or _phone !~ '^\+254[17][0-9]{8}$' then
      raise exception 'Use a Kenyan mobile number like 0712 345 678.' using errcode = 'P0001';
    end if;
    if coalesce(btrim(_new ->> 'name'), '') = '' then
      raise exception 'Add their name.' using errcode = 'P0001';
    end if;
    if _outcome <> 'spoke' then
      raise exception 'A new person is someone you spoke to.' using errcode = 'P0001';
    end if;

    select id into _pid from public.people where phone = _phone;
    if found then
      update public.people
         set full_name = coalesce(full_name, btrim(_new ->> 'name')),
             ward_id   = coalesce(ward_id, nullif(_new ->> 'ward_id', '')::uuid),
             segment   = coalesce(segment, nullif(btrim(_new ->> 'segment'), ''))
       where id = _pid;
    else
      insert into public.people (phone, full_name, ward_id, segment, language, source)
      values (_phone, btrim(_new ->> 'name'), nullif(_new ->> 'ward_id', '')::uuid,
              nullif(btrim(_new ->> 'segment'), ''), coalesce(nullif(_new ->> 'language', ''), 'sw'), 'door')
      returning id into _pid;
      _created := true;
      insert into public.person_events (person_id, kind, channel, detail, actor, created_at)
      values (_pid, 'added', 'door', 'Signed up at the door', auth.uid(), _at);
    end if;
  end if;

  select opted_out into _opted from public.people where id = _pid;
  if not found then
    raise exception 'That person is not on file, or you cannot see them.' using errcode = 'P0001';
  end if;

  _kind := case _outcome when 'spoke' then 'door_spoke'
                         when 'not_home' then 'door_not_home'
                         else 'door_refused' end;

  insert into public.person_events
    (person_id, kind, channel, detail, actor, created_at, client_id, lat, lng, accuracy_m, building_id)
  values (_pid, _kind, 'door',
          case when _outcome = 'spoke' then nullif(left(btrim(_issue), 120), '') end,
          auth.uid(), _at, _client_id, _lat, _lng, _acc, _bld);

  -- The house, too: every door knocked at a house is counted against it, with
  -- the ward of the person who answered (part 3 adds doors with no person).
  if _bld is not null then
    insert into public.house_visits
      (client_id, pc, ward_id, person_id, outcome, support, issue, actor, visited_at, lat, lng, accuracy_m)
    select _client_id, _bld, p.ward_id, _pid, _outcome,
           case when _outcome = 'spoke' then _support end,
           case when _outcome = 'spoke' then nullif(left(btrim(_issue), 120), '') end,
           auth.uid(), _at, _lat, _lng, _acc
      from public.people p
     where p.id = _pid
    on conflict (campaign_id, client_id) do nothing;
  end if;

  -- Pin the household: the door is their home. A later visit moves the pin;
  -- an earlier one synced late does not. A bare GPS point never replaces a
  -- building someone confirmed.
  if _bld is not null or _lat is not null then
    update public.people
       set building_id = coalesce(_bld, building_id),
           lat         = coalesce(_lat, lat),
           lng         = coalesce(_lng, lng),
           placed_at   = _at
     where id = _pid
       and (placed_at is null or placed_at <= _at)
       and (_bld is not null or building_id is null);
  end if;

  -- Speaking to someone, or being turned away, is contact; an empty house is not.
  if _outcome in ('spoke', 'refused') then
    update public.people
       set last_contacted_at = greatest(coalesce(last_contacted_at, _at), _at),
           -- The 1-5 door scale as a 0-100 score. Mirrors SUPPORT_SCORE in
           -- src/lib/people.ts: change both together.
           support_score = case when _outcome = 'spoke' and _support is not null
                                then (array[10, 30, 50, 75, 95])[_support]
                                else support_score end
     where id = _pid;
  end if;

  -- Consent given at the door, never over the top of a STOP.
  if (_sms or _wa or _call) and not coalesce(_opted, false) and _outcome = 'spoke' then
    update public.people
       set consent_sms      = consent_sms or _sms,
           consent_whatsapp = consent_whatsapp or _wa,
           consent_call     = consent_call or _call
     where id = _pid;
    _channels := concat_ws(', ',
      case when _sms then 'SMS' end, case when _wa then 'WhatsApp' end, case when _call then 'calls' end);
    insert into public.person_events (person_id, kind, channel, detail, actor, created_at)
    values (_pid, 'consent_given', 'door',
            left(format('Agreed to %s: %s', _channels, btrim(_consent_source)), 500), auth.uid(), _at);
  end if;

  return jsonb_build_object('repeat', false, 'person_id', _pid, 'created', _created,
                            'consent_blocked', (_sms or _wa or _call) and coalesce(_opted, false));
end;
$$;

revoke all on function public.record_door(uuid, uuid, jsonb, text, integer, text, jsonb, text, timestamptz, jsonb) from public, anon;
grant execute on function public.record_door(uuid, uuid, jsonb, text, integer, text, jsonb, text, timestamptz, jsonb) to authenticated;

-- 4. Doors per ward and per house, for the map's colours. In the last 30 days a
-- house takes its outcome (and its ward) from its latest visit and its score
-- from its latest visit that has one, so a later door where no one answered
-- keeps the score the house gave. house_doors() counts these per ward, with the
-- visits of the last seven days; it skips visits with no ward, so it returns
-- one row per ward. Ties between visits at the same moment go to the one
-- created last, then to the higher id. Both run as the caller, so an agent
-- counts their own.
create or replace function public.house_doors()
returns table (
  ward_id      uuid,
  knocked      integer,
  rated        integer,
  supporters   integer,
  doors_week   integer,
  agents_today uuid[]
)
language sql
stable
security invoker
set search_path = public
as $$
  with recent as (
    select v.pc, v.ward_id, v.outcome, v.support, v.visited_at, v.created_at, v.id
      from public.house_visits v
     where v.visited_at > now() - interval '30 days'
       and v.ward_id is not null
  ),
  latest as (
    select distinct on (r.pc) r.pc, r.ward_id, r.outcome
      from recent r
     order by r.pc, r.visited_at desc, r.created_at desc, r.id desc
  ),
  scored as (
    select distinct on (r.pc) r.pc, r.support
      from recent r
     where r.support is not null
     order by r.pc, r.visited_at desc, r.created_at desc, r.id desc
  ),
  per_ward as (
    select l.ward_id,
           count(*)::integer as knocked,
           (count(*) filter (where s.support is not null))::integer as rated,
           (count(*) filter (where s.support >= 4))::integer as supporters
      from latest l
      left join scored s on s.pc = l.pc
     group by l.ward_id
  ),
  week as (
    select v.ward_id,
           count(*)::integer as doors_week,
           coalesce(array_agg(distinct v.actor) filter (
             where v.actor is not null
               and v.visited_at >= date_trunc('day', now() at time zone 'Africa/Nairobi') at time zone 'Africa/Nairobi'
           ), '{}') as agents_today
      from public.house_visits v
     where v.visited_at > now() - interval '7 days'
       and v.ward_id is not null
     group by v.ward_id
  )
  select coalesce(p.ward_id, w.ward_id),
         coalesce(p.knocked, 0), coalesce(p.rated, 0), coalesce(p.supporters, 0),
         coalesce(w.doors_week, 0), coalesce(w.agents_today, '{}')
    from per_ward p
    full join week w on w.ward_id = p.ward_id
$$;

-- Each house in a ward with a visit in 30 days, ordered by plus code so a long
-- list pages: the outcome and time of its latest visit, and the score of its
-- latest visit that has one (null when none). Ties as in house_doors().
create or replace function public.ward_houses(_ward_id uuid)
returns table (pc text, visited_at timestamptz, outcome text, support smallint)
language sql
stable
security invoker
set search_path = public
as $$
  with recent as (
    select v.pc, v.visited_at, v.outcome, v.support, v.created_at, v.id
      from public.house_visits v
     where v.ward_id = _ward_id
       and v.visited_at > now() - interval '30 days'
  ),
  latest as (
    select distinct on (r.pc) r.pc, r.visited_at, r.outcome
      from recent r
     order by r.pc, r.visited_at desc, r.created_at desc, r.id desc
  ),
  scored as (
    select distinct on (r.pc) r.pc, r.support
      from recent r
     where r.support is not null
     order by r.pc, r.visited_at desc, r.created_at desc, r.id desc
  )
  select l.pc, l.visited_at, l.outcome, s.support
    from latest l
    left join scored s on s.pc = l.pc
   order by l.pc
$$;

revoke all on function public.house_doors() from public, anon;
grant execute on function public.house_doors() to authenticated;
revoke all on function public.ward_houses(uuid) from public, anon;
grant execute on function public.ward_houses(uuid) to authenticated;

-- 5. Door visits already on record with a house, copied in once. Only a
-- person's latest conversation at the door takes their score (the "last
-- score" a house shows); older visits keep none.
insert into public.house_visits
  (campaign_id, client_id, pc, ward_id, person_id, outcome, support, issue, actor,
   visited_at, lat, lng, accuracy_m)
select e.campaign_id,
       coalesce(e.client_id, e.id),
       e.building_id,
       p.ward_id,
       p.id,
       case e.kind when 'door_spoke' then 'spoke' when 'door_not_home' then 'not_home' else 'refused' end,
       case
         when e.kind = 'door_spoke'
          and e.id = (select l.id from public.person_events l
                       where l.person_id = e.person_id and l.kind = 'door_spoke'
                       order by l.created_at desc, l.id desc limit 1)
         then case when p.support_score >= 85 then 5
                   when p.support_score >= 62 then 4
                   when p.support_score >= 40 then 3
                   when p.support_score >= 20 then 2
                   when p.support_score > 0 then 1 end
       end,
       case when e.kind = 'door_spoke' then nullif(left(btrim(e.detail), 120), '') end,
       e.actor,
       e.created_at,
       e.lat,
       e.lng,
       e.accuracy_m
  from public.person_events e
  join public.people p on p.id = e.person_id
 where e.kind in ('door_spoke', 'door_not_home', 'door_refused')
   and e.building_id ~ '^[0-9A-Z+]{4,24}$'
on conflict (campaign_id, client_id) do nothing;

create or replace function public.groundwork_schema_version()
returns integer
language sql
immutable
as $$ select 23 $$;

revoke all on function public.groundwork_schema_version() from public, anon, authenticated;
grant execute on function public.groundwork_schema_version() to service_role;
```

- [ ] **Step 6: Write `src/lib/site/wards.ts` and use it in both loaders**

Create `src/lib/site/wards.ts`:

```ts
// Which wards a campaign's public site offers on its volunteer form. None for
// a presidential campaign: its supporters live anywhere in Kenya, while its
// workspace holds only the wards of its map area. None, too, when the list is
// too long for a page meant to load on 2G.

/** A ward list longer than this is too heavy a page for 2G: leave the question out. */
export const MAX_WARDS = 150;

export function siteWards<T>(level: string | null | undefined, wards: T[] | null): T[] {
  if (level === "president" || !wards || wards.length > MAX_WARDS) return [];
  return wards;
}
```

### Task 6: Server reads

**Files:**
- Create: `src/lib/houses.ts`
- Create: `src/lib/houses.functions.ts`
- Modify: `src/lib/map.functions.ts` (`MapConfig` gains `mapArea`)
- Modify: `src/lib/console.functions.ts` (`PersonRow` gains `house`)
- Modify: `tests/fake-supabase.ts` (`rpc()` takes `.range()`)
- Test: `tests/houses.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `house_doors()`, `ward_houses(_ward_id)`, `house_visits` and `campaigns.map_area` (Task 5); `BAND_COLOUR` from `src/lib/wardmap.ts`; `pageAll` from `src/lib/page-all.ts`; `PLUS_CODE` (Task 2); `requireSupabaseAuth`.
- Produces:
  - In `src/lib/houses.ts`: `type Outcome = "spoke" | "not_home" | "refused"`; `type Colour = "doors" | "support" | "vote"`; `type WardDoors = { wardId: string; knocked: number; rated: number; supporters: number; doorsWeek: number; agentsToday: string[] }`; `type HouseState = { pc: string; at: string; outcome: Outcome; support: number | null }`; `type HouseVisit = { at: string; agent: string | null; outcome: Outcome; support: number | null; issue: string | null; person: { id: string; name: string } | null }`; `type LegendItem = { label: string; colour: string }`; `type WardRef = { id: string; slug: string; name: string }`; `type DoorsFirstRow = { wardId; slug; name; houses; knocked; notKnocked }`; `type DoorTotals = { houses; knocked; rated; supporters; doorsWeek; agentsToday: number }`.
  - Constants `DOORS_FULL = 0.4`, `NO_DATA`, `NO_SCORE`, `PLAIN`, `BLOCK_M2 = 200`, `OUTCOME_LABEL`, `OUTCOME_COLOUR`.
  - `scaleColour(t: number): string`, `houseColour(colour: Colour, h: HouseState): string | null`, `wardShade(colour: Colour, houses: number, d: WardDoors | undefined): string`, `houseLegend(colour: Colour): LegendItem[]`, `wardLegend(colour: Colour): LegendItem[]`, `doorsFirst(wards: WardRef[], houses: Record<string, number>, doors: WardDoors[], max = 6): DoorsFirstRow[]`, `doorTotals(wards: { id: string; slug: string }[], houses: Record<string, number>, doors: WardDoors[]): DoorTotals`, `isOutcome(v: unknown): v is Outcome`, `toWardDoors(rows: unknown): WardDoors[]`, `toHouseStates(rows: unknown): HouseState[]`.
  - In `src/lib/houses.functions.ts`: `type Client` (a loose Supabase client); `readAreaDoors(sb: Client): Promise<WardDoors[] | null>`; `readWardHouses(sb: Client, wardId: string): Promise<HouseState[]>`; `readHouse(sb: Client, pc: string): Promise<HouseVisit[] | null>`; server functions `getAreaDoors()` (GET), `getWardHouses({ data: { wardId } })` and `getHouse({ data: { pc } })` (POST).
  - In `src/lib/map.functions.ts`: `MapConfig.mapArea: string | null` and `readMapArea(sb: Client): Promise<string | null>`.
  - `PersonRow.house: string | null` (the person's `people.building_id`).

Why every read fails soft: Lovable publishes a push before anyone applies its migration (Global Constraints). Until `20261012090000_voters_map.sql` is applied, `house_doors` doesn't exist and `campaigns.map_area` is missing, so `readAreaDoors` returns null ("Door counts aren't in yet"), `readWardHouses` returns nothing, and `readMapArea` returns null ("No map for this campaign yet"). Nothing else on the screen breaks (Review Focus 1 and 4).

- [ ] **Step 3: Write `src/lib/houses.ts`**

```ts
// The Voters map's colours and counts for houses and wards. A house takes the
// outcome, or the score, of its latest visit in 30 days; a ward is shaded by
// the share of its houses knocked, or by the supporters among its doors that
// gave a score. "Doors first" ranks wards by houses not yet knocked. Nothing
// is guessed: no data is grey, and the legend says so. Pure.

import { BAND_COLOUR } from "@/lib/wardmap";

export type Outcome = "spoke" | "not_home" | "refused";
export type Colour = "doors" | "support" | "vote";

/** A ward's doors: each house's latest visit in 30 days, and the visits of this week. */
export type WardDoors = {
  wardId: string;
  knocked: number;
  rated: number;
  supporters: number;
  doorsWeek: number;
  agentsToday: string[];
};

/** A house's latest visit in 30 days. */
export type HouseState = { pc: string; at: string; outcome: Outcome; support: number | null };

/** One visit at a house, for its card. */
export type HouseVisit = {
  at: string;
  agent: string | null;
  outcome: Outcome;
  support: number | null;
  issue: string | null;
  person: { id: string; name: string } | null;
};

export type LegendItem = { label: string; colour: string };

/** A ward with this share of its houses knocked in 30 days is shaded full green. */
export const DOORS_FULL = 0.4;
export const NO_DATA = "hsl(0, 0%, 80%)";
export const NO_SCORE = "hsl(150, 6%, 70%)";
export const PLAIN = "hsl(0, 0%, 100%)";
/** An outline at least this big is often several homes: a block of flats, a plot of rooms. */
export const BLOCK_M2 = 200;

export const OUTCOME_LABEL: Record<Outcome, string> = {
  spoke: "Spoke",
  not_home: "Not home",
  refused: "Refused",
};

export const OUTCOME_COLOUR: Record<Outcome, string> = {
  spoke: "hsl(142, 62%, 40%)",
  not_home: "hsl(200, 85%, 62%)",
  refused: "hsl(0, 72%, 55%)",
};

const RED = [0, 72, 52];
const AMBER = [40, 92, 52];
const GREEN = [142, 62, 40];

/** Red at 0, amber at 0.5, green at 1; clamped, and NaN reads as 0. */
export function scaleColour(t: number): string {
  const x = Number.isNaN(t) ? 0 : Math.min(Math.max(t, 0), 1);
  const [from, to, u] = x <= 0.5 ? [RED, AMBER, x / 0.5] : [AMBER, GREEN, (x - 0.5) / 0.5];
  const [h, s, l] = from.map((v, i) => Math.round(v + (to[i]! - v) * u));
  return `hsl(${h}, ${s}%, ${l}%)`;
}

/** A house's fill for the colour on show; null for the 2022 vote, which shades wards only. */
export function houseColour(colour: Colour, h: HouseState): string | null {
  if (colour === "doors") return OUTCOME_COLOUR[h.outcome];
  if (colour === "support") {
    const s = h.support;
    return s !== null && s >= 1 && s <= 5 ? BAND_COLOUR[s as 1 | 2 | 3 | 4 | 5] : NO_SCORE;
  }
  return null;
}

/** A ward's fill: the share of its houses knocked, or of its scored doors that support us. */
export function wardShade(colour: Colour, houses: number, d: WardDoors | undefined): string {
  if (colour === "doors")
    return houses > 0 ? scaleColour((d?.knocked ?? 0) / houses / DOORS_FULL) : NO_DATA;
  if (colour === "support") return d && d.rated > 0 ? scaleColour(d.supporters / d.rated) : NO_DATA;
  return NO_DATA;
}

const NOT_KNOCKED: LegendItem = { label: "Not knocked in 30 days", colour: PLAIN };

/** What a house's colour means, at street level. */
export function houseLegend(colour: Colour): LegendItem[] {
  if (colour === "support")
    return [
      { label: "1 against", colour: BAND_COLOUR[1] },
      { label: "3 undecided", colour: BAND_COLOUR[3] },
      { label: "5 strong", colour: BAND_COLOUR[5] },
      { label: "No score", colour: NO_SCORE },
      NOT_KNOCKED,
    ];
  if (colour === "doors")
    return [
      { label: OUTCOME_LABEL.spoke, colour: OUTCOME_COLOUR.spoke },
      { label: OUTCOME_LABEL.not_home, colour: OUTCOME_COLOUR.not_home },
      { label: OUTCOME_LABEL.refused, colour: OUTCOME_COLOUR.refused },
      NOT_KNOCKED,
    ];
  return [];
}

/** What a ward's colour means, above street level. */
export function wardLegend(colour: Colour): LegendItem[] {
  if (colour === "support")
    return [
      { label: "No supporters among doors scored", colour: scaleColour(0) },
      { label: "Half", colour: scaleColour(0.5) },
      { label: "All supporters", colour: scaleColour(1) },
      { label: "No doors scored", colour: NO_DATA },
    ];
  if (colour === "doors")
    return [
      { label: "No houses knocked", colour: scaleColour(0) },
      { label: "20% of houses", colour: scaleColour(0.5) },
      { label: "40% or more", colour: scaleColour(1) },
      { label: "No houses on the map", colour: NO_DATA },
    ];
  return [];
}

export type WardRef = { id: string; slug: string; name: string };
export type DoorsFirstRow = {
  wardId: string;
  slug: string;
  name: string;
  houses: number;
  knocked: number;
  notKnocked: number;
};

/** The wards with the most houses not knocked in 30 days; a ward with no house count is left out. */
export function doorsFirst(
  wards: WardRef[],
  houses: Record<string, number>,
  doors: WardDoors[],
  max = 6,
): DoorsFirstRow[] {
  const byWard = new Map(doors.map((d) => [d.wardId, d]));
  return wards
    .filter((w) => (houses[w.slug] ?? 0) > 0)
    .map((w) => {
      const total = houses[w.slug]!;
      const knocked = Math.min(byWard.get(w.id)?.knocked ?? 0, total);
      return {
        wardId: w.id,
        slug: w.slug,
        name: w.name,
        houses: total,
        knocked,
        notKnocked: total - knocked,
      };
    })
    .sort((a, b) => b.notKnocked - a.notKnocked || a.name.localeCompare(b.name))
    .slice(0, max);
}

export type DoorTotals = {
  houses: number;
  knocked: number;
  rated: number;
  supporters: number;
  doorsWeek: number;
  agentsToday: number;
};

/** The doors across some wards; an agent out in two of them counts once. */
export function doorTotals(
  wards: { id: string; slug: string }[],
  houses: Record<string, number>,
  doors: WardDoors[],
): DoorTotals {
  const ids = new Set(wards.map((w) => w.id));
  const inside = doors.filter((d) => ids.has(d.wardId));
  const sum = (f: (d: WardDoors) => number) => inside.reduce((t, d) => t + f(d), 0);
  return {
    houses: wards.reduce((t, w) => t + (houses[w.slug] ?? 0), 0),
    knocked: sum((d) => d.knocked),
    rated: sum((d) => d.rated),
    supporters: sum((d) => d.supporters),
    doorsWeek: sum((d) => d.doorsWeek),
    agentsToday: new Set(inside.flatMap((d) => d.agentsToday)).size,
  };
}

export const isOutcome = (v: unknown): v is Outcome =>
  v === "spoke" || v === "not_home" || v === "refused";

const count = (v: unknown): number | null =>
  typeof v === "number" && Number.isInteger(v) && v >= 0 ? v : null;

/** house_doors() rows; a row without a ward or with a bad count is left out. */
export function toWardDoors(rows: unknown): WardDoors[] {
  if (!Array.isArray(rows)) return [];
  const out: WardDoors[] = [];
  for (const r of rows as Record<string, unknown>[]) {
    const knocked = count(r["knocked"]);
    const rated = count(r["rated"]);
    const supporters = count(r["supporters"]);
    const doorsWeek = count(r["doors_week"]);
    if (typeof r["ward_id"] !== "string" || knocked === null || rated === null) continue;
    if (supporters === null || doorsWeek === null) continue;
    const agents = Array.isArray(r["agents_today"]) ? r["agents_today"] : [];
    out.push({
      wardId: r["ward_id"],
      knocked,
      rated,
      supporters,
      doorsWeek,
      agentsToday: agents.filter((a): a is string => typeof a === "string"),
    });
  }
  return out;
}

/** ward_houses() rows; a row without a house, a time or a known outcome is left out. */
export function toHouseStates(rows: unknown): HouseState[] {
  if (!Array.isArray(rows)) return [];
  const out: HouseState[] = [];
  for (const r of rows as Record<string, unknown>[]) {
    const s = r["support"];
    if (typeof r["pc"] !== "string" || typeof r["visited_at"] !== "string") continue;
    if (!isOutcome(r["outcome"])) continue;
    out.push({
      pc: r["pc"],
      at: r["visited_at"],
      outcome: r["outcome"],
      support: typeof s === "number" && s >= 1 && s <= 5 ? s : null,
    });
  }
  return out;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Write `src/lib/houses.functions.ts`**

The loose `Client` is the same shape `src/lib/atlas.functions.ts` uses: the generated Supabase types don't know `house_visits` or the two functions yet. Handlers pass `context.supabase as never`, as atlas does.

```ts
// The Voters map's doors, read as the signed-in person: row level security
// decides whose doors they count (the office every door in the campaign, an
// agent the doors they knocked). Every read fails soft: before the voters-map
// migration is applied there is no house_visits table, and the screen says the
// door counts aren't in yet instead of breaking.

import { createServerFn } from "@tanstack/react-start";

import { requireSupabaseAuth } from "@/integrations/supabase/auth-middleware";
import {
  isOutcome,
  toHouseStates,
  toWardDoors,
  type HouseState,
  type HouseVisit,
  type WardDoors,
} from "@/lib/houses";
import { pageAll } from "@/lib/page-all";
import { PLUS_CODE } from "@/lib/pluscode";

export type Client = {
  // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-explicit-any -- the Supabase builder, loosely: house_visits is newer than the generated types
  from: (table: string) => any;
  // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-explicit-any -- as above
  rpc: (fn: string, args?: Record<string, unknown>) => any;
};

const UUID = /^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{12}$/i;

/** Doors per ward in the campaign; null when they can't be read (the migration isn't in yet). */
export async function readAreaDoors(sb: Client): Promise<WardDoors[] | null> {
  const { data, error } = (await sb.rpc("house_doors")) as { data: unknown; error: unknown };
  if (error) return null;
  return toWardDoors(data);
}

/** Each house in a ward with a visit in 30 days, its latest; nothing when they can't be read. */
export async function readWardHouses(sb: Client, wardId: string): Promise<HouseState[]> {
  let failed = false;
  const rows = await pageAll<unknown>(async (from, to) => {
    const { data, error } = (await sb.rpc("ward_houses", { _ward_id: wardId }).range(from, to)) as {
      data: unknown[] | null;
      error: unknown;
    };
    if (error) failed = true;
    return { data: error ? [] : data };
  });
  return failed ? [] : toHouseStates(rows);
}

type VisitRow = {
  visited_at: string;
  actor: string | null;
  outcome: unknown;
  support: number | null;
  issue: string | null;
  person_id: string | null;
};

/** A house's visits, newest first (up to 50), with who knocked and who answered; null on a failed read. */
export async function readHouse(sb: Client, pc: string): Promise<HouseVisit[] | null> {
  const { data, error } = (await sb
    .from("house_visits")
    .select("visited_at, actor, outcome, support, issue, person_id")
    .eq("pc", pc)
    .order("visited_at", { ascending: false })
    .limit(50)) as { data: VisitRow[] | null; error: unknown };
  if (error) return null;
  const rows = (data ?? []).filter((r) => isOutcome(r.outcome));
  const actors = [...new Set(rows.map((r) => r.actor).filter((a): a is string => Boolean(a)))];
  const people = [...new Set(rows.map((r) => r.person_id).filter((p): p is string => Boolean(p)))];
  const [profiles, persons] = (await Promise.all([
    actors.length
      ? sb.from("profiles").select("user_id, full_name").in("user_id", actors)
      : { data: [] },
    people.length ? sb.from("people").select("id, full_name").in("id", people) : { data: [] },
  ])) as [
    { data: { user_id: string; full_name: string | null }[] | null },
    { data: { id: string; full_name: string | null }[] | null },
  ];
  const agentName = new Map(
    (profiles.data ?? []).map((p) => [p.user_id, p.full_name?.trim() || null]),
  );
  const personName = new Map((persons.data ?? []).map((p) => [p.id, p.full_name?.trim() || null]));
  return rows.map((r) => ({
    at: r.visited_at,
    agent: (r.actor && agentName.get(r.actor)) || null,
    outcome: r.outcome as HouseVisit["outcome"],
    support: typeof r.support === "number" ? r.support : null,
    issue: r.issue ?? null,
    person: r.person_id
      ? { id: r.person_id, name: personName.get(r.person_id) ?? "No name on file" }
      : null,
  }));
}

export const getAreaDoors = createServerFn({ method: "GET" })
  .middleware([requireSupabaseAuth])
  .handler(async ({ context }) => readAreaDoors(context.supabase as never));

export const getWardHouses = createServerFn({ method: "POST" })
  .middleware([requireSupabaseAuth])
  .inputValidator((input: { wardId: string }) => {
    if (!input?.wardId || !UUID.test(input.wardId)) throw new Error("Pick a ward.");
    return { wardId: input.wardId };
  })
  .handler(async ({ data, context }) => readWardHouses(context.supabase as never, data.wardId));

export const getHouse = createServerFn({ method: "POST" })
  .middleware([requireSupabaseAuth])
  .inputValidator((input: { pc: string }) => {
    const pc = String(input?.pc ?? "")
      .trim()
      .toUpperCase();
    if (!PLUS_CODE.test(pc)) throw new Error("That isn't a house on the map.");
    return { pc };
  })
  .handler(async ({ data, context }) => readHouse(context.supabase as never, data.pc));
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Give the map config the campaign's map area**

Replace the whole of `src/lib/map.functions.ts` with this (`getWardMap` is unchanged; Task 14 removes it):

```ts
// The ward map's two server calls: which basemap to draw with, and who is
// pinned where in a ward. Both need a signed-in team member.

import { createServerFn } from "@tanstack/react-start";

import { requireSupabaseAuth } from "@/integrations/supabase/auth-middleware";
import type { Client } from "@/lib/houses.functions";
import type { Outcome, WardMapRow } from "@/lib/wardmap";

export type MapConfig = {
  /**
   * MapTiler key for satellite and street tiles, set as MAPTILER_KEY in the
   * project's secrets (never in the repository). It is used by the browser
   * to fetch tiles, so restrict it to the site's domain in MapTiler. Without
   * it the map falls back to OpenFreeMap streets, with no satellite.
   */
  maptilerKey: string | null;
  /**
   * The atlas key of the area the campaign's Voters map covers
   * (campaigns.map_area; src/lib/map-areas.ts); null when it has none, or
   * before the voters-map migration is applied.
   */
  mapArea: string | null;
};

/** The campaign's map area; null on any failure, so the screen says there's no map yet. */
export async function readMapArea(sb: Client): Promise<string | null> {
  try {
    const { data: id, error } = (await sb.rpc("my_campaign")) as { data: unknown; error: unknown };
    if (error || typeof id !== "string") return null;
    const { data, error: failed } = (await sb
      .from("campaigns")
      .select("map_area")
      .eq("id", id)
      .maybeSingle()) as { data: { map_area?: unknown } | null; error: unknown };
    if (failed) return null;
    const area = data?.map_area;
    return typeof area === "string" && area ? area : null;
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

export const getMapConfig = createServerFn({ method: "GET" })
  .middleware([requireSupabaseAuth])
  .handler(async ({ context }): Promise<MapConfig> => {
    const key = process.env["MAPTILER_KEY"]?.trim();
    return {
      maptilerKey: key ? key : null,
      mapArea: await readMapArea(context.supabase as never),
    };
  });

const OUTCOMES = new Set<Outcome>(["spoke", "not_home", "refused"]);

export const getWardMap = createServerFn({ method: "POST" })
  .middleware([requireSupabaseAuth])
  .inputValidator((input: { wardId: string }) => {
    if (!input?.wardId) throw new Error("Pick a ward.");
    return input;
  })
  .handler(async ({ data, context }): Promise<WardMapRow[]> => {
    const { data: rows, error } = await context.supabase.rpc("ward_map", { _ward_id: data.wardId });
    if (error) throw new Error("Could not load the ward map.");
    return (rows ?? []).map((r) => ({
      id: r.id,
      name: r.full_name ?? "No name on file",
      phoneMasked: r.phone_masked,
      buildingId: r.building_id,
      lat: r.lat,
      lng: r.lng,
      support: r.support_score ?? 0,
      segment: r.segment,
      smsOk: Boolean(r.consent_sms) && !r.opted_out,
      optedOut: Boolean(r.opted_out),
      lastContactedAt: r.last_contacted_at,
      lastOutcome: OUTCOMES.has(r.last_outcome as Outcome) ? (r.last_outcome as Outcome) : null,
      lastVisitAt: r.last_visit_at,
      lastIssue: r.last_issue,
    }));
  });
```

- [ ] **Step 7: Let the fake's functions take `.range()`**

In `tests/fake-supabase.ts`, replace the whole `const rpc = (name: string, args: Row = {}) => { ... };` block (it ends just before `return { from, rpc, tables };`) with this. It runs the function at once, as before, and keeps `claim_morning_job` as it was; `await sb.rpc(...)` works as it did, and `.range(from, to)` slices a set the function returns, as PostgREST does:

```ts
  const call = (name: string, args: Row): Result => {
    if (name === "claim_morning_job") {
      const existing = rows("listening_jobs").find((j) => j["key"] === args["_key"]);
      if (
        existing &&
        (existing["status"] === "paused" ||
          String(existing["locked_until"] ?? "") > String(args["_now"]))
      )
        return { data: false, error: null };
      const lease = {
        key: args["_key"],
        status: "running",
        locked_until: new Date(Date.parse(String(args["_now"])) + 600_000).toISOString(),
      };
      if (existing) Object.assign(existing, lease);
      else rows("listening_jobs").push(lease);
      return { data: true, error: null };
    }
    const fn = rpcs[name];
    return fn
      ? { data: fn(args), error: null }
      : { data: null, error: { message: `no function ${name}` } };
  };

  /** Runs the function now, as the database would; .range() pages a set it returns. */
  const rpc = (name: string, args: Row = {}) => {
    const result = call(name, args);
    let page: [number, number] | null = null;
    const q = {
      range(from: number, to: number) {
        page = [from, to];
        return q;
      },
      then<T>(resolve: (r: Result) => T, reject?: (e: unknown) => T) {
        const r =
          page && Array.isArray(result.data)
            ? { ...result, data: result.data.slice(page[0], page[1] + 1) }
            : result;
        return Promise.resolve(r).then(resolve, reject);
      },
    };
    return q;
  };
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write `src/lib/voters-map.ts`**

```ts
// The Voters map's rules: which level a zoom shows, the area under a point,
// where the camera goes for an area or a house, what a click does (said on the
// map, as AGENTS.md asks), the constituency names drawn at the top level, and
// search. Pure, so the map component only draws.

import { wardAt, type Box, type LngLat, type WardShape } from "@/lib/geo";
import type { Top } from "@/lib/map-areas";
import { plusCodeCentre } from "@/lib/pluscode";
import type { Area } from "@/lib/voters-view";

export type Basemap = "satellite" | "streets";
export type Level = "area" | "constituency" | "ward" | "street";

export const MIN_ZOOM = 8;
export const CONSTITUENCY_ZOOM = 11;
export const WARD_ZOOM = 13;
export const STREET_ZOOM = 16;
export const MAX_ZOOM = 20;
/** A house opens close enough to see its neighbours. */
export const HOUSE_ZOOM_OPEN = 18;

/** The level a zoom shows. A constituency's map (Mathira) starts at the constituency. */
export function levelFor(zoom: number, top: Top): Level {
  if (zoom < CONSTITUENCY_ZOOM) return top === "constituency" ? "constituency" : "area";
  if (zoom < WARD_ZOOM) return "constituency";
  if (zoom < STREET_ZOOM) return "ward";
  return "street";
}

type WardRef = { slug: string; constituency: string };

/**
 * The area under a point at a level: the whole map at the top, the ward's
 * constituency (or the whole map, for a constituency's map) one level down,
 * else the ward. Null when no ward is under the point: keep the area on show.
 */
export function areaAt(
  level: Level,
  p: LngLat,
  shapes: WardShape[],
  wards: WardRef[],
  top: Top,
): Area | null {
  if (level === "area") return { level: "county" };
  const shape = wardAt(p, shapes);
  if (!shape) return null;
  if (level === "constituency") {
    if (top === "constituency") return { level: "county" };
    const name = wards.find((w) => w.slug === shape.slug)?.constituency;
    return name ? { level: "constituency", name } : null;
  }
  return { level: "ward", slug: shape.slug };
}

export type CameraTarget =
  | { kind: "point"; center: LngLat; zoom: number }
  | { kind: "box"; box: Box; minZoom: number; maxZoom: number };

const union = (boxes: Box[]): Box | null =>
  boxes.length
    ? {
        west: Math.min(...boxes.map((b) => b.west)),
        south: Math.min(...boxes.map((b) => b.south)),
        east: Math.max(...boxes.map((b) => b.east)),
        north: Math.max(...boxes.map((b) => b.north)),
      }
    : null;

/** Where the camera goes: a house, a ward, a constituency, else the whole map area. */
export function cameraFor(
  at: { area: Area; house?: string | null },
  wards: WardRef[],
  shapes: WardShape[],
  mapArea: { bounds: [number, number, number, number]; top: Top },
): CameraTarget {
  const centre = at.house ? plusCodeCentre(at.house) : null;
  if (centre) return { kind: "point", center: centre, zoom: HOUSE_ZOOM_OPEN };
  const { area } = at;
  if (area.level === "ward") {
    const box = shapes.find((s) => s.slug === area.slug)?.box;
    if (box) return { kind: "box", box, minZoom: WARD_ZOOM, maxZoom: STREET_ZOOM - 0.1 };
  }
  if (area.level === "constituency") {
    const slugs = new Set(wards.filter((w) => w.constituency === area.name).map((w) => w.slug));
    const box = union(shapes.filter((s) => slugs.has(s.slug)).map((s) => s.box));
    if (box) return { kind: "box", box, minZoom: CONSTITUENCY_ZOOM, maxZoom: WARD_ZOOM - 0.1 };
  }
  const [west, south, east, north] = mapArea.bounds;
  return {
    kind: "box",
    box: { west, south, east, north },
    minZoom: MIN_ZOOM,
    maxZoom: (mapArea.top === "constituency" ? WARD_ZOOM : CONSTITUENCY_ZOOM) - 0.1,
  };
}

/** What a click does at the level on show, said on the map. */
export function hintFor(level: Level, top: Top): string {
  if (level === "street") return "Click a house to open it";
  if (level === "ward") return "Zoom in, or click, to see the houses";
  if (level === "constituency" || top === "constituency") return "Click a ward to drop in";
  return "Click a ward to drop in, or a constituency's name";
}

export type LabelPoints = {
  type: "FeatureCollection";
  features: {
    type: "Feature";
    properties: { name: string };
    geometry: { type: "Point"; coordinates: LngLat };
  }[];
};

/** A point in the middle of each constituency's wards, for its name at the top level. */
export function constituencyLabels(shapes: WardShape[], wards: WardRef[]): LabelPoints {
  const boxes = new Map<string, Box[]>();
  for (const s of shapes) {
    const name = wards.find((w) => w.slug === s.slug)?.constituency;
    if (name) boxes.set(name, [...(boxes.get(name) ?? []), s.box]);
  }
  return {
    type: "FeatureCollection",
    features: [...boxes.entries()]
      .sort(([a], [b]) => a.localeCompare(b))
      .map(([name, bs]) => {
        const b = union(bs)!;
        return {
          type: "Feature" as const,
          properties: { name },
          geometry: {
            type: "Point" as const,
            coordinates: [(b.west + b.east) / 2, (b.south + b.north) / 2] as LngLat,
          },
        };
      }),
  };
}

export type SearchHit =
  | { kind: "ward"; slug: string; label: string; sub: string }
  | { kind: "constituency"; name: string; label: string; sub: string }
  | {
      kind: "person";
      id: string;
      label: string;
      sub: string;
      house: string | null;
      wardSlug: string | null;
    }
  | { kind: "place"; label: string; sub: string; center: LngLat };

type SearchWard = { slug: string; name: string; constituency: string };
type SearchPerson = {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  phone: string;
  ward: string | null;
  house: string | null;
};

const fold = (s: string) =>
  s
    .toLowerCase()
    .replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, " ")
    .trim();

/**
 * Wards and constituencies by name, and people by name, or by their number
 * when four or more digits are typed. At most `max` of each kind.
 */
export function searchHits(
  q: string,
  wards: SearchWard[],
  people: SearchPerson[],
  top: Top,
  max = 5,
): SearchHit[] {
  const needle = fold(q);
  if (!needle) return [];
  const digits = q.replace(/\D/g, "");
  // Typed the local way (0733…) or the international way (254733…), numbers are kept +254….
  const local = digits.replace(/^(?:254|0)/, "");
  const out: SearchHit[] = [];
  out.push(
    ...wards
      .filter((w) => fold(w.name).includes(needle))
      .slice(0, max)
      .map((w) => ({
        kind: "ward" as const,
        slug: w.slug,
        label: `${w.name} ward`,
        sub: top === "constituency" ? "Ward" : w.constituency,
      })),
  );
  if (top === "county") {
    const names = [...new Set(wards.map((w) => w.constituency))].sort();
    out.push(
      ...names
        .filter((n) => fold(n).includes(needle))
        .slice(0, max)
        .map((n) => ({ kind: "constituency" as const, name: n, label: n, sub: "Constituency" })),
    );
  }
  const slugOf = new Map(wards.map((w) => [w.name, w.slug]));
  out.push(
    ...people
      .filter((p) =>
        digits.length >= 4
          ? p.phone.replace(/\D/g, "").includes(local)
          : fold(p.name).includes(needle),
      )
      .slice(0, max)
      .map((p) => ({
        kind: "person" as const,
        id: p.id,
        label: p.name,
        sub: p.ward ? `${p.ward} ward` : "No ward on file",
        house: p.house,
        wardSlug: p.ward ? (slugOf.get(p.ward) ?? null) : null,
      })),
  );
  return out;
}

/** MapTiler's search for places and streets inside the map area; null without a key or for under 3 letters. */
export function geocodeUrl(
  q: string,
  key: string | null,
  bounds: [number, number, number, number],
): string | null {
  const text = q.trim();
  if (!key || text.length < 3) return null;
  const params = new URLSearchParams({
    key,
    bbox: bounds.join(","),
    country: "ke",
    limit: "5",
  });
  return `https://api.maptiler.com/geocoding/${encodeURIComponent(text)}.json?${params.toString()}`;
}

/** Places from a MapTiler search reply; anything without a name and a centre is left out. */
export function placeHits(json: unknown): SearchHit[] {
  const features = (json as { features?: unknown } | null)?.features;
  if (!Array.isArray(features)) return [];
  const out: SearchHit[] = [];
  for (const f of features as Record<string, unknown>[]) {
    const c = f["center"];
    const label = typeof f["text"] === "string" ? f["text"] : null;
    if (!label || !Array.isArray(c) || typeof c[0] !== "number" || typeof c[1] !== "number")
      continue;
    const full = typeof f["place_name"] === "string" ? f["place_name"] : "";
    out.push({
      kind: "place",
      label,
      sub: full.startsWith(`${label}, `) ? full.slice(label.length + 2) : full || "Place",
      center: [c[0], c[1]],
    });
  }
  return out;
}
```

- Consumes: `openArchive`, `gunzip`, `Archive` (Task 1); `decodeTile`, `HOUSE_ZOOM`, `tilesAround`, `mergeHouses` (Task 2); `MapArea`, `mapAreaFor` (Task 3); `MapConfig.mapArea` and `getMapConfig` (Task 6); `resolvePlace` and `useFix` (unchanged, in `useDoorPlace.ts`).
- Produces:
  - `readRange(url, offset, length, fetcher = fetch): Promise<Uint8Array>`, `houseArchive(url, fetcher = fetch): Promise<Archive>`, `housesInTile(area: MapArea, x: number, y: number, fetcher = fetch): Promise<Building[]>` in `src/lib/house-source.ts` (Task 10's map reads tiles through `houseArchive` too).
  - `type HouseStatus = "none" | "loading" | "ready"`, `houseStatus(area: MapArea | null, fix: LngLat | null, pending: boolean, count: number): HouseStatus` and `useHousesAround(area, fix): { buildings: Building[]; status: HouseStatus }` in `src/hooks/useHousesAround.ts`.
  - `useDoorPlace(area: MapArea | null): DoorPlaceState`, where `DoorPlaceState` gains `houses: { buildings: Building[]; status: HouseStatus }`; `DoorPlace({ door })`.

The phone reads the zoom-16 tiles within 120 metres of its fix (the 80 m picker plus room for a vague fix). React Query keeps each tile for the session (`staleTime` and `gcTime` Infinity), so a street walked into a dead zone still has its houses; an open that fails without signal is forgotten and tried again. Outside the area's bounds, or with no map area, the status is "none" and the visit is placed by GPS, as before (Review Focus 1 and 2). `getWardMap`, `buildingsIndexQuery` and `wardBuildingsQuery` still exist after this task; Task 14 removes them.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test**

Create `tests/door-place.test.ts`. It builds a one-tile house file in memory and serves it from a fake host, so nothing leaves the process:

```ts
// Checks for "Which house am I at?": whether the houses around the phone are
// known (no map area, no fix, outside the area), and reading a house file a
// tile at a time by range requests, from a host that honours ranges and one
// that doesn't, and again after a failed first try. Nothing leaves this
// process. Run from the repository root:
//   npx tsx --tsconfig tsconfig.json tests/door-place.test.ts

import { gzipSync } from "node:zlib";

import { houseStatus } from "@/hooks/useHousesAround";
import type { Ring } from "@/lib/geo";
import { houseArchive, housesInTile, readRange } from "@/lib/house-source";
import { encodeTile, toTileHouse, toTileSpace } from "@/lib/house-tiles";
import { mapAreaFor, type MapArea } from "@/lib/map-areas";
import { writeArchive } from "@/lib/pmtiles";

let pass = 0;
let fail = 0;

function eq(name: string, got: unknown, want: unknown) {
  const g = JSON.stringify(got);
  const w = JSON.stringify(want);
  if (g === w) pass++;
  else {
    fail++;
    console.log(`FAIL ${name}\n  got  ${g}\n  want ${w}`);
  }
}

const NAIROBI = mapAreaFor("nairobi")!;
const KILELESHWA: [number, number] = [36.760926, -1.265323];
const THIKA: [number, number] = [37.0693, -1.0388];

eq(
  "no map area: no houses, the visit is placed by GPS",
  houseStatus(null, KILELESHWA, false, 0),
  "none",
);
eq("no fix yet: still finding where you are", houseStatus(NAIROBI, null, false, 0), "loading");
eq("a fix outside the area: not on the house map", houseStatus(NAIROBI, THIKA, false, 0), "none");
eq("inside, before the first tile arrives", houseStatus(NAIROBI, KILELESHWA, true, 0), "loading");
eq(
  "inside, with houses (more tiles may follow)",
  houseStatus(NAIROBI, KILELESHWA, true, 12),
  "ready",
);
eq(
  "inside, every tile read, even none with houses",
  houseStatus(NAIROBI, KILELESHWA, false, 0),
  "ready",
);

// A house file with one tile holding one Kileleshwa house.
const PC = "6GCRPQM6+V9F4";
const RING: Ring = [
  [36.760986, -1.265294],
  [36.760894, -1.265264],
  [36.760866, -1.265352],
  [36.760958, -1.265382],
  [36.760986, -1.265294],
];
const [tx, ty] = toTileSpace(RING[0]!);
const x = Math.floor(tx);
const y = Math.floor(ty);
const FILE = writeArchive(
  [{ z: 16, x, y, data: encodeTile(x, y, [toTileHouse(PC, [RING])!]) }],
  { minZoom: 16, maxZoom: 16, bounds: NAIROBI.bounds, center: [36.82, -1.29, 16], metadata: {} },
  (b) => new Uint8Array(gzipSync(b)),
);

/** A host for FILE: honouring ranges (206), ignoring them (200), or failing the first n reads. */
function host(mode: "ranges" | "whole", failFirst = 0) {
  let reads = 0;
  const f = async (_url: string, init?: RequestInit) => {
    reads++;
    if (reads <= failFirst) throw new TypeError("Failed to fetch");
    const range = new Headers(init?.headers).get("Range") ?? "";
    const m = /^bytes=(\d+)-(\d+)$/.exec(range);
    if (mode === "whole" || !m) return new Response(FILE, { status: 200 });
    const from = Number(m[1]);
    const to = Math.min(Number(m[2]), FILE.length - 1);
    return new Response(FILE.subarray(from, to + 1), { status: 206 });
  };
  return { fetch: f, reads: () => reads };
}

{
  const h = host("ranges");
  const b = await readRange("mem://a", 10, 5, h.fetch);
  eq("a range from a host that honours them", [...b], [...FILE.subarray(10, 15)]);
  const w = await readRange("mem://a", 10, 5, host("whole").fetch);
  eq("and from one that sends the whole file, cut", [...w], [...FILE.subarray(10, 15)]);
}

const areaAt = (url: string): MapArea => ({ ...NAIROBI, houses: url });
{
  const h = host("ranges");
  const houses = await housesInTile(areaAt("mem://ranges"), x, y, h.fetch);
  eq(
    "the house in its tile, by its plus code",
    houses.map((b) => b.id),
    [PC],
  );
  eq(
    "a tile the file doesn't have holds no houses",
    await housesInTile(areaAt("mem://ranges"), x + 1, y, h.fetch),
    [],
  );
  const before = h.reads();
  await housesInTile(areaAt("mem://ranges"), x, y, h.fetch);
  eq("the file is opened once: a tile is one more read", h.reads() - before, 1);
}
{
  const h = host("whole");
  eq(
    "a host that ignores ranges still works",
    (await housesInTile(areaAt("mem://whole"), x, y, h.fetch)).map((b) => b.id),
    [PC],
  );
}
{
  const h = host("ranges", 1);
  let first = "no error";
  try {
    await houseArchive("mem://flaky", h.fetch);
  } catch (e) {
    first = e instanceof Error ? e.message : String(e);
  }
  eq("no signal: the open fails", first, "Failed to fetch");
  eq(
    "signal back: it is tried again, not remembered as failed",
    (await housesInTile(areaAt("mem://flaky"), x, y, h.fetch)).map((b) => b.id),
    [PC],
  );
}

console.log(`${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);
process.exit(fail ? 1 : 0);
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write `src/lib/house-source.ts`**

```ts
// The browser's reader for a house file (public/tiles/houses-<id>.pmtiles):
// the header and directory once, then one tile per request, by HTTP range.
// Each file is opened once a session; an open that fails (no signal) is
// forgotten, so the next read tries again.

import type { Building } from "@/lib/geo";
import { decodeTile, HOUSE_ZOOM } from "@/lib/house-tiles";
import type { MapArea } from "@/lib/map-areas";
import { gunzip, openArchive, type Archive } from "@/lib/pmtiles";

type Fetch = (url: string, init?: RequestInit) => Promise<Response>;

const archives = new Map<string, Promise<Archive>>();

/** length bytes of url from offset. A host that ignores the range sends the whole file, which is cut. */
export async function readRange(
  url: string,
  offset: number,
  length: number,
  fetcher: Fetch = fetch,
): Promise<Uint8Array> {
  const res = await fetcher(url, {
    headers: { Range: `bytes=${offset}-${offset + length - 1}` },
  });
  if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`Could not read ${url} (${res.status}).`);
  const bytes = new Uint8Array(await res.arrayBuffer());
  return res.status === 206 ? bytes : bytes.subarray(offset, offset + length);
}

/** The house file at url, opened once a session. */
export function houseArchive(url: string, fetcher: Fetch = fetch): Promise<Archive> {
  let opened = archives.get(url);
  if (!opened) {
    opened = openArchive((o, l) => readRange(url, o, l, fetcher), gunzip);
    archives.set(url, opened);
    opened.catch(() => archives.delete(url));
  }
  return opened;
}

/** The houses in one zoom-16 tile of an area's house file; none where the file has no tile. */
export async function housesInTile(
  area: MapArea,
  x: number,
  y: number,
  fetcher: Fetch = fetch,
): Promise<Building[]> {
  const archive = await houseArchive(area.houses, fetcher);
  const bytes = await archive.getTile(HOUSE_ZOOM, x, y);
  return bytes ? decodeTile(x, y, bytes) : [];
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Write `src/hooks/useHousesAround.ts`**

`combine` sits outside the hook so React Query keeps the merged list while no tile changes; `useDoorPlace`'s `useMemo` depends on it.

```ts
// The houses within reach of the phone: the zoom-16 tiles of the area's house
// file around the GPS fix. Each tile is read while there is signal and kept
// for the session, so a street walked into a dead zone still finds its houses.

import { useQueries, type UseQueryResult } from "@tanstack/react-query";

import { inBox, type Building, type LngLat } from "@/lib/geo";
import { housesInTile } from "@/lib/house-source";
import { mergeHouses, tilesAround } from "@/lib/house-tiles";
import type { MapArea } from "@/lib/map-areas";

/** The 80 m picker, plus room for a vague fix. */
const AROUND_M = 120;

export type HouseStatus = "none" | "loading" | "ready";

/**
 * Whether the houses around the phone are known: none without a map area or
 * outside it, loading until there is a fix and a first tile, else ready.
 */
export function houseStatus(
  area: MapArea | null,
  fix: LngLat | null,
  pending: boolean,
  count: number,
): HouseStatus {
  if (!area) return "none";
  if (!fix) return "loading";
  const [west, south, east, north] = area.bounds;
  if (!inBox(fix, { west, south, east, north })) return "none";
  if (pending && count === 0) return "loading";
  return "ready";
}

// Outside the hook, so React Query keeps the merged list while no tile changes.
const combine = (results: UseQueryResult<Building[]>[]) => ({
  buildings: mergeHouses(results.map((r) => r.data ?? [])),
  pending: results.some((r) => r.isPending),
});

export function useHousesAround(
  area: MapArea | null,
  fix: LngLat | null,
): { buildings: Building[]; status: HouseStatus } {
  const inside = houseStatus(area, fix, false, 0) === "ready";
  const tiles = inside && fix ? tilesAround(fix, AROUND_M) : [];
  const { buildings, pending } = useQueries({
    queries: tiles.map((t) => ({
      queryKey: ["house-tile", area?.id, t.x, t.y],
      queryFn: () => housesInTile(area!, t.x, t.y),
      staleTime: Infinity,
      gcTime: Infinity,
      retry: 1,
    })),
    combine,
  });
  return { buildings, status: houseStatus(area, fix, pending, buildings.length) };
}
```

In words:
- `useDoorPlace.ts`: imports `useHousesAround`, `HouseStatus` and `MapArea`; `DoorPlaceState` gains `houses`; `useDoorPlace(area)` calls `useHousesAround(area, fix)` with the fix as `[lng, lat]` and resolves the place against `houses.buildings`.
- `DoorPlace.tsx`: takes only `door`, reads `door.houses`, and says "loading the houses around you…" and "This place isn't on the campaign's house map, so the visit is placed by GPS."
- `FieldApp.tsx`: reads the map config under the key `["map-config"]` (the Voters map uses the same key), gives `VisitSheet` the `area` (`mapAreaFor(mapConfig?.mapArea)`), and drops the imports of `toBuildings`, `Building`, `buildingsIndexQuery` and `wardBuildingsQuery`.
- `field.ts`: the `buildingId` comment names the house file.

### Task 9: Preview harness (scratchpad, not committed)

The Voters screen needs a signed-in team member and the database, which a session doesn't have. This harness runs the real console layout and screens in Vite with the server functions running in the browser against `tests/fake-supabase.ts`, seeded with Sakaja's campaign, Nairobi's 85 wards, people placed at real Kileleshwa houses, and 900 door visits over 40 days. Tasks 10 to 13 are checked in it by eye and by screenshot. Nothing here is committed.

**Files (all in `$SCRATCH/harness/`):** `index.html`, `vite.config.ts`, `fake-start.ts`, `fake-client.ts`, `fake-auth-middleware.ts`, `fake-db.ts`, `main.tsx`, `kileleshwa-houses.json`, a `node_modules` link, and `kileleshwa-tiles.ts` (only needed without Task 4's files). A screenshot script, `$SCRATCH/shots/shoot.mjs`.

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `fakeSupabase` (`tests/fake-supabase.ts`, with Task 6's `.range()`); `house_doors` and `ward_houses` as Task 5 defines them (worked out in `fake-db.ts`); the routes `src/routes/_authenticated/route.tsx`, `voters.tsx` and `field.tsx`; `public/` (ward shapes and Task 4's house files).
- Produces: `http://127.0.0.1:5198/voters` and `/field`, signed in as Sakaja's campaign manager (`my_campaign_role` = "manager"); the real ward ids are uuids, as `getWardHouses` checks.

```ts
// A preview of the console's Voters and field screens with no backend: server
// functions run in the browser against an in-memory Supabase (fake-db.ts).
// Not committed. Run: REPO=<checkout> npx vite --config <this file>
import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";

import tailwind from "@tailwindcss/vite";
import react from "@vitejs/plugin-react";
import { defineConfig } from "vite";

const HERE = fileURLToPath(new URL(".", import.meta.url));
const REPO = process.env["REPO"] ?? process.cwd();

export default defineConfig({
  root: HERE,
  publicDir: `${REPO}/public`,
  plugins: [react(), tailwind()],
  resolve: {
    alias: [
      { find: "@tanstack/react-start", replacement: `${HERE}fake-start.ts` },
      { find: "@/integrations/supabase/auth-middleware", replacement: `${HERE}fake-auth-middleware.ts` },
      { find: "@/integrations/supabase/client", replacement: `${HERE}fake-client.ts` },
      { find: /^@\//, replacement: `${REPO}/src/` },
      { find: /^@repo\//, replacement: `${REPO}/` },
    ],
    dedupe: ["react", "react-dom", "@tanstack/react-router", "@tanstack/react-query"],
  },
  define: { "process.env": JSON.stringify({ MAPTILER_KEY: "" }) },
  server: { port: 5198, strictPort: true, host: "127.0.0.1", fs: { allow: [REPO, HERE] } },
});
```

```ts
// @tanstack/react-start for the harness: a server function runs in the
// browser with the in-memory Supabase as its client.
import { fake, USER_ID } from "./fake-db";

type Handler = (ctx: { data: unknown; context: Record<string, unknown> }) => unknown;

export function createServerFn(_opts?: unknown) {
  let check: ((input: unknown) => unknown) | null = null;
  const builder = {
    middleware: () => builder,
    inputValidator: (f: (input: unknown) => unknown) => {
      check = f;
      return builder;
    },
    validator: (f: (input: unknown) => unknown) => {
      check = f;
      return builder;
    },
    handler: (fn: Handler) => async (opts?: { data?: unknown }) => {
      const data = check ? check(opts?.data) : opts?.data;
      return fn({ data, context: { supabase: fake, userId: USER_ID } });
    },
  };
  return builder;
}

export const useServerFn = <T,>(f: T): T => f;
export const createMiddleware = () => ({ server: () => ({}) });
export const createCsrfMiddleware = () => ({});
export const createStart = () => ({});
```
