`$SCRATCH/harness/fake-db.ts`:

```ts
// The harness's in-memory Supabase: Sakaja's campaign, Nairobi's 85 wards,
// people placed at real Kileleshwa houses, and door visits over 40 days, with
// the database functions the Voters map calls worked out here. Seeded, so
// every run shows the same doors.
import wardsGeo from "@repo/public/geo/nairobi-wards.json";
import { fakeSupabase } from "@repo/tests/fake-supabase";

import kileleshwa from "./kileleshwa-houses.json";

export const USER_ID = "00000000-0000-4000-8000-0000000000c3";
const AGENT_ID = "00000000-0000-4000-8000-0000000000b2";
const CAMPAIGN = "ca000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000002";
const DAY = 86_400_000;
const NOW = Date.now();

let state = 7;
const rnd = () => (state = (state * 16_807) % 2_147_483_647) / 2_147_483_647;
const pick = <T,>(xs: T[]): T => xs[Math.floor(rnd() * xs.length)]!;
const uuid = (group: string, n: number) =>
  `00000000-0000-4000-${group}-${String(n).padStart(12, "0")}`;

type Feature = { properties: { slug: string; name: string; constituency: string } };
const wards = (wardsGeo as { features: Feature[] }).features.map((f, i) => ({
  id: uuid("9000", i + 1),
  campaign_id: CAMPAIGN,
  slug: f.properties.slug,
  name: f.properties.name,
  constituency: f.properties.constituency,
  registered_voters: 18_000 + Math.round(rnd() * 30_000),
  target_votes: 9_000 + Math.round(rnd() * 8_000),
  supporters: Math.round(rnd() * 4_000),
}));
const kile = wards.find((w) => w.slug === "kileleshwa")!;
const pcs = kileleshwa as string[];

const FIRST = ["Wanjiru", "Brian", "Faith", "Kevin", "Mercy", "Dennis", "Grace", "Peter", "Joy", "Collins"];
const LAST = ["Kamau", "Otieno", "Chebet", "Mutua", "Wambui", "Omondi", "Njoroge", "Akinyi", "Kiprono", "Muthoni"];
const people = Array.from({ length: 260 }, (_, i) => ({
  id: uuid("a000", i + 1),
  campaign_id: CAMPAIGN,
  full_name: `${FIRST[i % FIRST.length]} ${LAST[Math.floor(i / FIRST.length) % LAST.length]}`,
  phone: `+2547${String(10_000_000 + i * 7_919).slice(0, 8)}`,
  ward_id: i < 200 ? kile.id : pick(wards).id,
  segment: null,
  source: pick(["door", "sms", "import"]),
  support_score: pick([0, 10, 30, 50, 75, 95]),
  tags: [],
  language: "sw",
  consent_sms: i % 3 === 0,
  consent_whatsapp: false,
  consent_call: false,
  opted_out: false,
  last_contacted_at: new Date(NOW - Math.round(rnd() * 40) * DAY).toISOString(),
  created_at: "2026-09-01T08:00:00Z",
  notes: null,
  building_id: i < 200 ? pcs[(i * 37) % pcs.length]! : null,
  lat: null,
  lng: null,
}));

const OUTCOMES = ["spoke", "spoke", "spoke", "not_home", "not_home", "refused"] as const;
const houseVisits: Record<string, unknown>[] = [];
for (let i = 0; i < 900; i++) {
  const inKile = i < 600;
  const outcome = pick([...OUTCOMES]);
  houseVisits.push({
    id: uuid("b000", i + 1),
    campaign_id: CAMPAIGN,
    client_id: uuid("c000", i + 1),
    pc: inKile ? pcs[(i * 13) % pcs.length]! : `6GCR${String(1000 + i)}+22`,
    ward_id: inKile ? kile.id : wards[(i * 7) % wards.length]!.id,
    person_id: null,
    outcome,
    support: outcome === "spoke" ? 1 + Math.floor(rnd() * 5) : null,
    issue: outcome === "spoke" ? pick(["Water", "Roads", "Security", "Jobs", null]) : null,
    actor: pick([USER_ID, AGENT_ID]),
    visited_at: new Date(NOW - Math.round(rnd() * 40 * 24) * 3_600_000).toISOString(),
  });
}

type Visit = { pc: string; ward_id: string; outcome: string; support: number | null; visited_at: string; actor: string };
const visits = houseVisits as unknown as Visit[];
const since = (days: number) => new Date(NOW - days * DAY).toISOString();
function latestPerHouse(rows: Visit[]): Visit[] {
  const latest = new Map<string, Visit>();
  for (const v of rows.filter((r) => r.visited_at > since(30)))
    if (!latest.has(v.pc) || latest.get(v.pc)!.visited_at < v.visited_at) latest.set(v.pc, v);
  return [...latest.values()];
}

function houseDoors() {
  const byWard = new Map<string, Record<string, unknown>>();
  const row = (id: string) => {
    if (!byWard.has(id))
      byWard.set(id, { ward_id: id, knocked: 0, rated: 0, supporters: 0, doors_week: 0, agents_today: [] });
    return byWard.get(id)! as Record<string, number> & { agents_today: string[] };
  };
  for (const v of latestPerHouse(visits)) {
    const r = row(v.ward_id);
    r.knocked += 1;
    if (v.outcome === "spoke" && v.support !== null) r.rated += 1;
    if (v.outcome === "spoke" && (v.support ?? 0) >= 4) r.supporters += 1;
  }
  const today = new Date(new Date().toDateString()).toISOString();
  for (const v of visits.filter((x) => x.visited_at > since(7))) {
    const r = row(v.ward_id);
    r.doors_week += 1;
    if (v.visited_at >= today && !r.agents_today.includes(v.actor)) r.agents_today.push(v.actor);
  }
  return [...byWard.values()];
}

function wardHouses(args: Record<string, unknown>) {
  return latestPerHouse(visits.filter((v) => v.ward_id === args["_ward_id"]))
    .sort((a, b) => a.pc.localeCompare(b.pc))
    .map((v) => ({ pc: v.pc, visited_at: v.visited_at, outcome: v.outcome, support: v.support }));
}

export const fake = fakeSupabase(
  {
    campaigns: [
      {
        id: CAMPAIGN,
        slug: "sakaja",
        name: "Sakaja 2027",
        candidate: "Johnson Sakaja",
        seat: "Governor · Nairobi",
        level: "governor",
        host: null,
        owns_channels: true,
        map_area: "nairobi",
        created_at: "2026-09-28T00:00:00Z",
      },
    ],
    wards,
    people,
    person_events: [],
    segments: [],
    house_visits: houseVisits,
    profiles: [
      { user_id: USER_ID, full_name: "Harness Manager" },
      { user_id: AGENT_ID, full_name: "Brian Otieno" },
    ],
  },
  {
    my_campaign: () => CAMPAIGN,
    my_campaign_role: () => "manager",
    request_campaign_access: () => "ok",
    house_doors: () => houseDoors(),
    ward_houses: (args) => wardHouses(args),
  },
);
```

```tsx
// The harness's app: the console layout and the screens being previewed,
// re-parented under a client-only root the way src/routeTree.gen.ts does it.
import "@/styles.css";

import { QueryClient, QueryClientProvider } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { createRootRoute, createRouter, Outlet, RouterProvider } from "@tanstack/react-router";
import { createRoot } from "react-dom/client";

import { Route as AuthedRoute } from "@/routes/_authenticated/route";
import { Route as FieldRoute } from "@/routes/_authenticated/field";
import { Route as VotersRoute } from "@/routes/_authenticated/voters";

const queryClient = new QueryClient({ defaultOptions: { queries: { retry: false } } });
const root = createRootRoute({ component: () => <Outlet /> });
const authed = AuthedRoute.update({ id: "/_authenticated", getParentRoute: () => root } as never);
const voters = VotersRoute.update({ id: "/voters", path: "/voters", getParentRoute: () => authed } as never);
const field = FieldRoute.update({ id: "/field", path: "/field", getParentRoute: () => authed } as never);
const router = createRouter({
  routeTree: root.addChildren([authed.addChildren([voters, field])]),
  context: { queryClient },
});

createRoot(document.getElementById("root")!).render(
  <QueryClientProvider client={queryClient}>
    <RouterProvider router={router} />
  </QueryClientProvider>,
);
```

```js
import { chromium } from "playwright";
const [, , url, out, width, height, waitMs] = process.argv;
const browser = await chromium.launch({ executablePath: "/opt/pw-browsers/chromium-1194/chrome-linux/chrome", args: ["--use-gl=swiftshader", "--enable-webgl", "--ignore-gpu-blocklist"] });
const page = await browser.newPage({ viewport: { width: Number(width), height: Number(height ?? 900) } });
const logs = [];
page.on("console", (m) => { if (m.type() === "error" || m.type() === "warning") logs.push(`${m.type()}: ${m.text()}`.slice(0, 300)); });
page.on("pageerror", (e) => logs.push(`pageerror: ${String(e).slice(0, 300)}`));
await page.goto(url, { waitUntil: "load", timeout: 120000 });
await page.waitForTimeout(Number(waitMs ?? 6000));
await page.screenshot({ path: out });
console.log(out);
for (const l of logs.slice(0, 15)) console.log(l);
await browser.close();
```

`$SCRATCH/harness/kileleshwa-tiles.ts`:

```ts
// A Kileleshwa-only houses-nairobi.pmtiles, from public/geo/buildings/kileleshwa.json,
// for previewing before Task 4's house files exist. Run from the repository root:
//   npx tsx --tsconfig tsconfig.json <harness>/kileleshwa-tiles.ts <out folder>
import { mkdirSync, readFileSync, writeFileSync } from "node:fs";
import { gzipSync } from "node:zlib";

import { tilesFor } from "@/lib/house-build";
import { toTileHouse, type TileHouse } from "@/lib/house-tiles";
import { mapAreaFor } from "@/lib/map-areas";
import { writeArchive } from "@/lib/pmtiles";

const out = process.argv[2];
if (!out) throw new Error("Say where to write: an empty folder used as the harness's public folder.");
type Outlines = { features: { properties: { pc: string }; geometry: { coordinates: [number, number][][] } }[] };
const file = JSON.parse(readFileSync("public/geo/buildings/kileleshwa.json", "utf8")) as Outlines;
const houses = file.features
  .map((f) => toTileHouse(f.properties.pc, f.geometry.coordinates))
  .filter((h): h is TileHouse => h !== null);
const area = mapAreaFor("nairobi")!;
const bytes = writeArchive(
  tilesFor(houses),
  { minZoom: 16, maxZoom: 16, bounds: area.bounds, center: [36.79, -1.27, 16], metadata: { name: "Houses: Kileleshwa only" } },
  (b) => new Uint8Array(gzipSync(b)),
);
mkdirSync(`${out}/tiles`, { recursive: true });
writeFileSync(`${out}/tiles/houses-nairobi.pmtiles`, bytes);
writeFileSync(
  `${out}/tiles/houses-nairobi.json`,
  JSON.stringify({ area: "nairobi", source: "Kileleshwa only", minConfidence: 0.65, built: "preview", total: houses.length, wards: { kileleshwa: houses.length } }),
);
console.log(`${houses.length} houses, ${(bytes.length / 1024).toFixed(0)} KiB`);
```

Run from the repository root: `npx -y tsx --tsconfig tsconfig.json "$SCRATCH/harness/kileleshwa-tiles.ts" "$SCRATCH/harness-public"`. Expected: `11708 houses, 256 KiB`. ### Task 10: The map canvas

**Files:**
- Create: `src/components/gw/voters/VotersCanvas.tsx`
- Harness only (not committed): `$SCRATCH/harness/canvas-preview.tsx`, a `/canvas` route in `$SCRATCH/harness/main.tsx`, `$SCRATCH/shots/click.mjs`

A change from the design: the canvas is a new file, not `git mv` of `WardMap.tsx`. Today's screen (`VotersMap.tsx`) imports `WardMap` until Task 11 replaces it, and moving it would leave this task's commit failing the type check. Task 14 deletes `WardMap.tsx` and `VotersMap.tsx`; `VotersCanvas` keeps `WardMap`'s style switching, zoom controls, MapTiler logo and scale.

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `houseArchive` (Task 8); `LAYER` (Task 2); `MAP_AREAS`, `MapArea` (Task 3); `ringAreaM2`, `Ring`, `WardShape` (Task 2's `geo.ts`); from Task 7 `areaAt`, `cameraFor`, `constituencyLabels`, `levelFor`, `CONSTITUENCY_ZOOM`, `STREET_ZOOM`, `MIN_ZOOM`, `MAX_ZOOM`, `Basemap`, `CameraTarget`, `LabelPoints`, `Level`; `Area` (voters-view).
- Produces: `type WardsGeo` and `VotersCanvas(props)` with props `mapArea`, `maptilerKey`, `basemap`, `wards: WardsGeo`, `shapes: WardShape[]`, `wardInfo: { slug: string; constituency: string }[]`, `area: Area`, `wardFill: Record<string, string>`, `houseColours: Map<string, string>`, `house: string | null`, `camera: { seq: number; target: CameraTarget } | null`, `padding: { bottom: number }`, `onMoved(area: Area | null, level: Level)`, `onPick(area: Area)`, `onHouse(pc: string | null, footprintM2: number | null)`, `onLevel(level: Level)`, `onTilesRefused()`, `children`.

How it behaves:
- One map for every level: zoom 8 to 20 (AGENTS.md: MapLibre's own pan, wheel and pinch, bounded), bounds the area's plus a quarter on each side, no rotation.
- Houses: the vector source `gw-houses` reads `houses://<area id>/{z}/{x}/{y}`, a protocol registered once that returns a tile of the area's house file through `houseArchive` (an empty tile where there is none). Zoom 16 tiles only, stretched to 20; `promoteId: "pc"`, so a house's colour is feature state by plus code. The basemap's own buildings (OpenMapTiles' `building` layer) are hidden: our houses are the buildings.
- Wards: filled by `wardFill` below zoom 16, outlined always (the ward on show in the accent colour); at street level the other wards are dimmed. Ward names show from zoom 10.5 (a county's map; all zooms for a constituency's) to 16; a county's constituency names show below 11 and can be clicked.
- A click goes one level down: a constituency name or a ward (`onPick`), at ward level eases to zoom 16.5 where clicked, at street level a house (`onHouse`, with its footprint) or nothing.
- The camera: a new `camera.seq` flies to `camera.target` (a box is fitted, then its zoom clamped to the target's range), marked `{ app: true }`; `moveend` ignores those and reports any other move as `onMoved(areaAt(...), level)`. `padding.bottom` keeps the phone's sheet (Task 13) out of the map's centre.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write `src/components/gw/voters/VotersCanvas.tsx`**

```tsx
// The Voters map: one MapLibre map for every level, from the campaign's whole
// area down to a single house. Wards are shaded by the colour on show; from
// zoom 16 every house in the area is drawn from its house file (a tile at a
// time, through the "houses" protocol) and coloured by its latest visit. The
// level follows the zoom. When the person moves the map, the area on show
// follows its centre; when the app moves it (a link, a search, the fit
// button), it flies there and says nothing back. Draws only: the rules are in
// src/lib/voters-map.ts.

import "maplibre-gl/dist/maplibre-gl.css";

import { useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState, type ReactNode } from "react";
import type { GeoJSONSource, LngLatBoundsLike, Map as MlMap, MapGeoJSONFeature } from "maplibre-gl";
// MapLibre looks for its worker next to its own file, which a bundle doesn't
// keep. Vite builds the worker as its own asset and this is its URL.
import maplibreWorker from "maplibre-gl/dist/maplibre-gl-worker.mjs?worker&url";

import { ringAreaM2, type Ring, type WardShape } from "@/lib/geo";
import { houseArchive } from "@/lib/house-source";
import { LAYER } from "@/lib/house-tiles";
import { MAP_AREAS, type MapArea } from "@/lib/map-areas";
import {
  areaAt,
  cameraFor,
  CONSTITUENCY_ZOOM,
  constituencyLabels,
  levelFor,
  MAX_ZOOM,
  MIN_ZOOM,
  STREET_ZOOM,
  type Basemap,
  type CameraTarget,
  type LabelPoints,
  type Level,
} from "@/lib/voters-map";
import type { Area } from "@/lib/voters-view";

export type WardsGeo = {
  type: "FeatureCollection";
  features: {
    type: "Feature";
    properties: { slug: string; name: string; constituency?: string };
    geometry: { type: "Polygon" | "MultiPolygon"; coordinates: unknown };
  }[];
};

type Props = {
  mapArea: MapArea;
  maptilerKey: string | null;
  basemap: Basemap;
  wards: WardsGeo;
  shapes: WardShape[];
  wardInfo: { slug: string; constituency: string }[];
  /** The area on show: its ward gets the accent line, and at street level the others are dimmed. */
  area: Area;
  /** A fill per ward slug, for the levels above the street. */
  wardFill: Record<string, string>;
  /** A fill per house plus code; houses not listed are drawn plain. */
  houseColours: Map<string, string>;
  /** The house whose card is open. */
  house: string | null;
  /** Where the app sends the camera; a new seq sends it again. */
  camera: { seq: number; target: CameraTarget } | null;
  /** Room the panel takes at the bottom (the sheet on phones), kept out of the map's centre. */
  padding: { bottom: number };
  /** The person moved the map: the area under its centre (null outside every ward), and the level. */
  onMoved: (area: Area | null, level: Level) => void;
  /** A click on a ward or a constituency's name, one level down. */
  onPick: (area: Area) => void;
  /** A click at street level: a house and its footprint in m², or null for none. */
  onHouse: (pc: string | null, footprintM2: number | null) => void;
  onLevel: (level: Level) => void;
  /** The basemap provider refused us (a bad or restricted key). */
  onTilesRefused: () => void;
  /** Controls, hint and legend drawn over the map. */
  children?: ReactNode;
};

const EMPTY = { type: "FeatureCollection" as const, features: [] };
const OPENFREEMAP = "https://tiles.openfreemap.org/styles/liberty";
const NO_WARD = "__none__";

function styleUrl(basemap: Basemap, key: string | null): string {
  if (!key) return OPENFREEMAP;
  const k = encodeURIComponent(key);
  return basemap === "satellite"
    ? `https://api.maptiler.com/maps/hybrid/style.json?key=${k}`
    : `https://api.maptiler.com/maps/streets-v2/style.json?key=${k}`;
}

let housesProtocol = false;

/** houses://<area id>/{z}/{x}/{y}: a tile of the area's house file, read by range request. */
function addHousesProtocol(ml: typeof import("maplibre-gl")): void {
  if (housesProtocol) return;
  housesProtocol = true;
  ml.addProtocol("houses", async (params) => {
    const m = /^houses:\/\/([a-z0-9-]+)\/(\d+)\/(\d+)\/(\d+)$/.exec(params.url);
    const area = m ? MAP_AREAS.find((a) => a.id === m[1]) : undefined;
    if (!m || !area) return { data: new ArrayBuffer(0) };
    const archive = await houseArchive(area.houses);
    const bytes = await archive.getTile(Number(m[2]), Number(m[3]), Number(m[4]));
    return { data: bytes ? bytes.slice().buffer : new ArrayBuffer(0) };
  });
}

/** Our layers go on top of whichever basemap is showing; re-added after every style change. */
function addOverlays(map: MlMap, basemap: Basemap, mapArea: MapArea): void {
  const light = basemap === "streets";
  const ink = "hsl(158, 15%, 10%)";
  const quiet = light ? "hsla(158, 15%, 10%, 0.45)" : "hsla(0, 0%, 100%, 0.6)";
  const accent = "hsl(15, 77%, 52%)";
  const text = light ? ink : "hsl(0, 0%, 100%)";
  const halo = light ? "hsl(0, 0%, 100%)" : ink;

  // Our houses are the buildings: the basemap's own (OpenMapTiles' "building" layer) go.
  for (const l of map.getStyle().layers ?? [])
    if ((l as { "source-layer"?: string })["source-layer"] === "building")
      map.setLayoutProperty(l.id, "visibility", "none");

  map.addSource("gw-wards", {
    type: "geojson",
    data: EMPTY,
    promoteId: "slug",
    attribution: "Wards: geoBoundaries (CC BY 4.0)",
  });
  map.addSource("gw-houses", {
    type: "vector",
    tiles: [`houses://${mapArea.id}/{z}/{x}/{y}`],
    minzoom: STREET_ZOOM,
    maxzoom: STREET_ZOOM,
    bounds: mapArea.bounds,
    promoteId: "pc",
    attribution: "Houses: Google Open Buildings v3 (CC BY 4.0)",
  });
  map.addSource("gw-constituencies", { type: "geojson", data: EMPTY });

  map.addLayer({
    id: "gw-wards-fill",
    type: "fill",
    source: "gw-wards",
    maxzoom: STREET_ZOOM,
    paint: {
      "fill-color": ["to-color", ["coalesce", ["feature-state", "fill"], "hsla(0, 0%, 0%, 0)"]],
      "fill-opacity": ["case", ["boolean", ["feature-state", "sel"], false], 0.3, 0.55],
    },
  });
  map.addLayer({
    id: "gw-houses-fill",
    type: "fill",
    source: "gw-houses",
    "source-layer": LAYER,
    minzoom: STREET_ZOOM,
    paint: {
      "fill-color": ["to-color", ["coalesce", ["feature-state", "col"], "hsl(0, 0%, 100%)"]],
      "fill-opacity": [
        "case",
        ["boolean", ["feature-state", "has"], false],
        0.8,
        light ? 0.35 : 0.18,
      ],
    },
  });
  map.addLayer({
    id: "gw-houses-line",
    type: "line",
    source: "gw-houses",
    "source-layer": LAYER,
    minzoom: STREET_ZOOM + 0.5,
    paint: {
      "line-color": [
        "case",
        ["boolean", ["feature-state", "focus"], false],
        accent,
        light ? "hsla(158, 15%, 10%, 0.55)" : "hsla(0, 0%, 100%, 0.7)",
      ],
      "line-width": ["case", ["boolean", ["feature-state", "focus"], false], 3, 0.7],
    },
  });
  // At street level, every ward but the one on show is dimmed.
  map.addLayer({
    id: "gw-wards-mask",
    type: "fill",
    source: "gw-wards",
    minzoom: STREET_ZOOM,
    filter: ["==", ["get", "slug"], NO_WARD],
    paint: { "fill-color": ink, "fill-opacity": 0.45 },
  });
  map.addLayer({
    id: "gw-wards-line",
    type: "line",
    source: "gw-wards",
    paint: {
      "line-color": ["case", ["boolean", ["feature-state", "sel"], false], accent, quiet],
      "line-width": ["case", ["boolean", ["feature-state", "sel"], false], 3, 1],
    },
  });
  map.addLayer({
    id: "gw-wards-label",
    type: "symbol",
    source: "gw-wards",
    minzoom: mapArea.top === "county" ? CONSTITUENCY_ZOOM - 0.5 : 0,
    maxzoom: STREET_ZOOM,
    layout: {
      "text-field": ["get", "name"],
      "text-font": ["Noto Sans Regular"],
      "text-size": 11,
      "text-max-width": 8,
    },
    paint: { "text-color": text, "text-halo-color": halo, "text-halo-width": 1.2 },
  });
  if (mapArea.top === "county")
    map.addLayer({
      id: "gw-constituency-label",
      type: "symbol",
      source: "gw-constituencies",
      maxzoom: CONSTITUENCY_ZOOM,
      layout: {
        "text-field": ["get", "name"],
        "text-font": ["Noto Sans Regular"],
        "text-size": 13,
        "text-transform": "uppercase",
        "text-letter-spacing": 0.06,
        "text-max-width": 7,
      },
      paint: { "text-color": text, "text-halo-color": halo, "text-halo-width": 1.6 },
    });
}

function hasLayer(map: MlMap, id: string): boolean {
  try {
    return Boolean(map.getLayer(id));
  } catch {
    return false;
  }
}

/** A house's footprint from its drawn outline, in whole m². */
function footprint(f: MapGeoJSONFeature): number | null {
  const g = f.geometry;
  const polygons: Ring[][] =
    g.type === "Polygon"
      ? [g.coordinates as Ring[]]
      : g.type === "MultiPolygon"
        ? (g.coordinates as Ring[][])
        : [];
  const first = polygons[0];
  if (!first?.[0]) return null;
  const holes = first.slice(1).reduce((s, r) => s + ringAreaM2(r), 0);
  return Math.round(ringAreaM2(first[0]) - holes);
}

function withMargin([w, s, e, n]: [number, number, number, number]): LngLatBoundsLike {
  const dx = (e - w) * 0.25;
  const dy = (n - s) * 0.25;
  return [
    [w - dx, s - dy],
    [e + dx, n + dy],
  ];
}

/** The app moves the camera: flown, and marked so moveend doesn't report it as the person's move. */
function fly(map: MlMap, t: CameraTarget, duration = 800): void {
  if (t.kind === "point") {
    map.flyTo({ center: t.center, zoom: t.zoom, duration, essential: true }, { app: true });
    return;
  }
  const c = map.cameraForBounds(
    [
      [t.box.west, t.box.south],
      [t.box.east, t.box.north],
    ],
    { padding: 32 },
  );
  if (!c?.center || c.zoom === undefined) return;
  const zoom = Math.min(Math.max(c.zoom, t.minZoom), t.maxZoom);
  map.flyTo({ center: c.center, zoom, duration, essential: true }, { app: true });
}

export function VotersCanvas(props: Props) {
  const host = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const mapRef = useRef<MlMap | null>(null);
  const latest = useRef(props);
  latest.current = props;
  // loaded: the first style is up; styleRev bumps after every style load so
  // data and states are put back on the new basemap.
  const [loaded, setLoaded] = useState(false);
  const [styleRev, setStyleRev] = useState(0);
  const [zoomLevel, setZoomLevel] = useState(MIN_ZOOM);
  const labels: LabelPoints = useMemo(
    () => constituencyLabels(props.shapes, props.wardInfo),
    [props.shapes, props.wardInfo],
  );

  useEffect(() => {
    let dead = false;
    let map: MlMap | null = null;
    void import("maplibre-gl").then((ml) => {
      if (dead || !host.current) return;
      ml.setWorkerUrl(maplibreWorker);
      addHousesProtocol(ml);
      const p = latest.current;
      const start =
        p.camera?.target ?? cameraFor({ area: p.area }, p.wardInfo, p.shapes, p.mapArea);
      map = new ml.Map({
        container: host.current,
        style: styleUrl(p.basemap, p.maptilerKey),
        ...(start.kind === "point"
          ? { center: start.center, zoom: start.zoom }
          : {
              bounds: [
                [start.box.west, start.box.south],
                [start.box.east, start.box.north],
              ] as LngLatBoundsLike,
              fitBoundsOptions: { padding: 32, maxZoom: start.maxZoom },
            }),
        maxBounds: withMargin(p.mapArea.bounds),
        attributionControl: { compact: true },
        dragRotate: false,
        pitchWithRotate: false,
        minZoom: MIN_ZOOM,
        maxZoom: MAX_ZOOM,
      });
      map.touchZoomRotate.disableRotation();
      map.addControl(new ml.ScaleControl({ maxWidth: 110, unit: "metric" }), "bottom-right");
      setZoomLevel(map.getZoom());
      map.on("style.load", () => {
        if (!map) return;
        addOverlays(map, latest.current.basemap, latest.current.mapArea);
        setStyleRev((r) => r + 1);
        setLoaded(true);
      });
      let level = levelFor(map.getZoom(), p.mapArea.top);
      map.on("zoom", () => {
        if (!map) return;
        const z = map.getZoom();
        setZoomLevel(z);
        const next = levelFor(z, latest.current.mapArea.top);
        if (next !== level) {
          level = next;
          latest.current.onLevel(next);
        }
      });
      map.on("moveend", (e) => {
        if (!map || (e as { app?: boolean }).app) return;
        const q = latest.current;
        const at = levelFor(map.getZoom(), q.mapArea.top);
        const c = map.getCenter();
        q.onMoved(areaAt(at, [c.lng, c.lat], q.shapes, q.wardInfo, q.mapArea.top), at);
      });
      map.on("error", (e) => {
        const status = (e.error as { status?: number } | undefined)?.status;
        if (status === 401 || status === 403) latest.current.onTilesRefused();
      });
      map.on("click", (e) => {
        if (!map) return;
        const q = latest.current;
        const at = levelFor(map.getZoom(), q.mapArea.top);
        if (at === "street") {
          const hit = hasLayer(map, "gw-houses-fill")
            ? map.queryRenderedFeatures(e.point, { layers: ["gw-houses-fill"] })[0]
            : undefined;
          const pc = hit ? String(hit.properties?.["pc"] ?? "") : "";
          q.onHouse(pc || null, hit && pc ? footprint(hit) : null);
          return;
        }
        if (at === "ward") {
          // The person's move: moveend says where it ended up.
          map.easeTo({ center: e.lngLat, zoom: STREET_ZOOM + 0.5, duration: 600 });
          return;
        }
        const label =
          at === "area" && hasLayer(map, "gw-constituency-label")
            ? map.queryRenderedFeatures(e.point, { layers: ["gw-constituency-label"] })[0]
            : undefined;
        const name = label ? String(label.properties?.["name"] ?? "") : "";
        if (name) {
          q.onPick({ level: "constituency", name });
          return;
        }
        const w = hasLayer(map, "gw-wards-fill")
          ? map.queryRenderedFeatures(e.point, { layers: ["gw-wards-fill"] })[0]
          : undefined;
        const slug = w ? String(w.properties?.["slug"] ?? "") : "";
        if (slug) q.onPick({ level: "ward", slug });
      });
      for (const layer of ["gw-houses-fill", "gw-wards-fill", "gw-constituency-label"]) {
        map.on("mouseenter", layer, () => {
          if (map) map.getCanvas().style.cursor = "pointer";
        });
        map.on("mouseleave", layer, () => {
          if (map) map.getCanvas().style.cursor = "";
        });
      }
      mapRef.current = map;
    });
    return () => {
      dead = true;
      map?.remove();
      mapRef.current = null;
    };
  }, []);

  // A different basemap: reload the style in full (a diff would skip style.load);
  // our layers are added back on style.load.
  useEffect(() => {
    const map = mapRef.current;
    if (!map) return;
    map.setStyle(styleUrl(props.basemap, props.maptilerKey), { diff: false });
  }, [props.basemap, props.maptilerKey]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const map = mapRef.current;
    if (!map || !styleRev) return;
    (map.getSource("gw-wards") as GeoJSONSource | undefined)?.setData(props.wards as never);
    (map.getSource("gw-constituencies") as GeoJSONSource | undefined)?.setData(labels as never);
  }, [styleRev, props.wards, labels]);

  const shownWard = props.area.level === "ward" ? props.area.slug : null;
  useEffect(() => {
    const map = mapRef.current;
    if (!map || !styleRev) return;
    for (const f of props.wards.features) {
      const slug = f.properties.slug;
      map.setFeatureState(
        { source: "gw-wards", id: slug },
        { fill: props.wardFill[slug] ?? "", sel: slug === shownWard },
      );
    }
    if (hasLayer(map, "gw-wards-mask"))
      map.setFilter("gw-wards-mask", ["!=", ["get", "slug"], shownWard ?? NO_WARD]);
  }, [styleRev, props.wards, props.wardFill, shownWard]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const map = mapRef.current;
    if (!map || !styleRev || !map.getSource("gw-houses")) return;
    const source = { source: "gw-houses", sourceLayer: LAYER };
    map.removeFeatureState(source);
    for (const [id, col] of props.houseColours)
      map.setFeatureState({ ...source, id }, { col, has: true });
    if (props.house) map.setFeatureState({ ...source, id: props.house }, { focus: true });
  }, [styleRev, props.houseColours, props.house]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const map = mapRef.current;
    if (!map || !loaded || !props.camera) return;
    fly(map, props.camera.target);
    // Only a new seq sends the camera again.
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, [loaded, props.camera?.seq]);

  useEffect(() => {
    mapRef.current?.setPadding({ top: 0, right: 0, left: 0, bottom: props.padding.bottom });
  }, [props.padding.bottom, loaded]);

  function zoom(by: number) {
    const map = mapRef.current;
    if (!map) return;
    map.easeTo({ zoom: Math.max(MIN_ZOOM, Math.min(MAX_ZOOM, map.getZoom() + by)), duration: 240 });
  }

  function fit() {
    const map = mapRef.current;
    if (!map) return;
    fly(map, cameraFor({ area: props.area }, props.wardInfo, props.shapes, props.mapArea), 600);
  }

  return (
    <div className="vmap">
      <div
        ref={host}
        className="vmap-canvas"
        aria-label={`Map of ${props.mapArea.name}: its wards, and every house from zoom 16`}
      />
      {!loaded && <div className="vmap-loading meta">Loading the map…</div>}
      {props.children}
      <div className="vglass vzoom" aria-label="Map zoom controls">
        <button
          type="button"
          aria-label="Zoom in"
          title="Zoom in"
          disabled={!loaded || zoomLevel >= MAX_ZOOM - 0.01}
          onClick={() => zoom(1)}
        >
          +
        </button>
        <button
          type="button"
          aria-label="Zoom out"
          title="Zoom out"
          disabled={!loaded || zoomLevel <= MIN_ZOOM + 0.01}
          onClick={() => zoom(-1)}
        >
          −
        </button>
        <button
          type="button"
          aria-label="Show the whole area on show"
          title="Show the whole area on show"
          onClick={fit}
        >
          <span aria-hidden="true">⌖</span>
        </button>
      </div>
      {props.maptilerKey && (
        <a
          className="vlogo"
          href="https://www.maptiler.com"
          target="_blank"
          rel="noopener noreferrer"
        >
          <img
            src="https://api.maptiler.com/resources/logo.svg"
            alt="MapTiler"
            width="67"
            height="20"
          />
        </a>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}
```

Write `$SCRATCH/harness/canvas-preview.tsx`, the canvas alone with Nairobi's wards shaded by doors and Kileleshwa's houses coloured by their last visit:

```tsx
// The map canvas alone, for Task 10: Nairobi's wards shaded by doors, Kileleshwa's
// houses coloured by their last visit. ?at=street opens on a house.
import { useQuery } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { useMemo, useState } from "react";

import { VotersCanvas, type WardsGeo } from "@/components/gw/voters/VotersCanvas";
import { wardShapes } from "@/lib/geo";
import { getJson } from "@/lib/geo-files";
import { houseColour, wardShade, type HouseState, type WardDoors } from "@/lib/houses";
import { readAreaDoors, readWardHouses } from "@/lib/houses.functions";
import { mapAreaFor, type HouseCounts } from "@/lib/map-areas";
import { cameraFor, hintFor, levelFor, type Level } from "@/lib/voters-map";
import type { Area } from "@/lib/voters-view";

import { fake } from "./fake-db";

const AREA = mapAreaFor("nairobi")!;

export function CanvasPreview() {
  const { data: geo } = useQuery({ queryKey: ["geo"], queryFn: () => getJson<WardsGeo>(AREA.wards) });
  const { data: counts } = useQuery({ queryKey: ["counts"], queryFn: () => getJson<HouseCounts>(AREA.counts) });
  const { data: doors } = useQuery({ queryKey: ["doors"], queryFn: () => readAreaDoors(fake as never) });
  const wards = (fake.tables["wards"] ?? []) as { id: string; slug: string; constituency: string }[];
  const kile = wards.find((w) => w.slug === "kileleshwa")!;
  const { data: houses } = useQuery({ queryKey: ["houses"], queryFn: () => readWardHouses(fake as never, kile.id) });
  const street = new URLSearchParams(location.search).get("at") === "street";
  const [area, setArea] = useState<Area>(street ? { level: "ward", slug: "kileleshwa" } : { level: "county" });
  const [level, setLevel] = useState<Level>(street ? "street" : "area");
  const [log, setLog] = useState<string[]>([]);
  const shapes = useMemo(() => (geo ? wardShapes(geo) : []), [geo]);
  const wardFill = useMemo(() => {
    const byId = new Map((doors ?? []).map((d: WardDoors) => [d.wardId, d]));
    return Object.fromEntries(wards.map((w) => [w.slug, wardShade("doors", counts?.wards[w.slug] ?? 0, byId.get(w.id))]));
  }, [doors, counts, wards]);
  const houseColours = useMemo(
    () => new Map((houses ?? []).map((h: HouseState) => [h.pc, houseColour("doors", h)!])),
    [houses],
  );
  if (!geo || !counts) return <p>Loading…</p>;
  const camera = street
    ? { seq: 1, target: cameraFor({ area, house: "6GCRPQM6+V9F4" }, wards, shapes, AREA) }
    : null;
  return (
    <div style={{ position: "fixed", inset: 0, display: "grid", gridTemplateRows: "1fr auto" }}>
      <div style={{ position: "relative" }}>
        <VotersCanvas
          mapArea={AREA}
          maptilerKey={null}
          basemap="streets"
          wards={geo}
          shapes={shapes}
          wardInfo={wards}
          area={area}
          wardFill={wardFill}
          houseColours={houseColours}
          house={null}
          camera={camera}
          padding={{ bottom: 0 }}
          onMoved={(a, l) => {
            if (a) setArea(a);
            setLog((x) => [`moved: ${JSON.stringify(a)} ${l}`, ...x].slice(0, 4));
          }}
          onPick={(a) => setLog((x) => [`pick: ${JSON.stringify(a)}`, ...x].slice(0, 4))}
          onHouse={(pc, m2) => setLog((x) => [`house: ${pc} ${m2} m²`, ...x].slice(0, 4))}
          onLevel={setLevel}
          onTilesRefused={() => undefined}
        >
          <div className="vglass vhint" role="status">{hintFor(level, AREA.top)}</div>
        </VotersCanvas>
      </div>
      <pre id="log" style={{ margin: 0, fontSize: 12 }}>{`level ${level} · ${JSON.stringify(area)} · ${levelFor(0, "county")}\n${log.join("\n")}`}</pre>
    </div>
  );
}
```

Write `$SCRATCH/shots/click.mjs`, which opens a page, clicks a point and prints the preview's log:

```js
import { chromium } from "playwright";
const [, , url, out, x, y, waitMs] = process.argv;
const browser = await chromium.launch({ executablePath: "/opt/pw-browsers/chromium-1194/chrome-linux/chrome", args: ["--use-gl=swiftshader", "--enable-webgl", "--ignore-gpu-blocklist"] });
const page = await browser.newPage({ viewport: { width: 1280, height: 800 } });
const errors = [];
page.on("pageerror", (e) => errors.push(String(e).slice(0, 200)));
await page.goto(url, { waitUntil: "load" });
await page.waitForTimeout(Number(waitMs ?? 10000));
await page.mouse.click(Number(x), Number(y));
await page.waitForTimeout(2500);
await page.screenshot({ path: out });
console.log((await page.textContent("#log")) ?? "");
for (const e of errors) console.log("pageerror", e);
await browser.close();
```

- `node click.mjs "http://127.0.0.1:5198/canvas?at=street" click-house.png 665 240 12000`. Expected: a log line `house: 6GCRPQM6+... <n> m²` (the house clicked).

### Task 11: The screen

**Files:**
- Modify: `src/lib/voters-view.ts` (the address keeps `tab`, `house`, `colour` and `base`; old `view` links; a `Home` for titles and crumbs)
- Rewrite: `src/routes/_authenticated/voters.tsx`
- Create: `src/components/gw/voters/MapPanel.tsx`
- Modify: `src/components/gw/voters/VotersCanvas.tsx` (two fixes, below)
- Modify: `src/components/gw/overview/LiveOverview.tsx` and `src/routes/_authenticated/canvassing.tsx` (their links to Voters)
- Modify: `src/styles/groundwork.css` (the screen's layout)
- Delete: `src/components/gw/VotersMap.tsx`, `src/components/gw/WardMap.tsx`, `src/components/gw/voters/DoorsView.tsx`, `src/components/gw/voters/WardsView.tsx`
- Test: `tests/voters-view.test.ts` (rewritten)

Changes from the design, found by running it in the harness:
- **The old screen's components go here, not in Task 14.** `VotersMap.tsx` links with `view: "people"`, which stops type-checking once the address has no `view`, and `DoorsView.tsx` imports `walkNote`. Nothing else uses them or `WardMap.tsx`. `walkNote` goes too: its only user was the Doors view's walk list, which the map replaces. Task 14 still removes `getWardMap`, the building-outline queries and files, and the script that made them.
- **`nextSearch` cleans the old address first.** The router hands back keys the screen doesn't keep, so an old `view=people` left in the URL reopened the People tab when Map was pressed.
- **Two fixes to `VotersCanvas`.** `setPadding` jumps the camera: on load it stopped the first flight (a ward link on a phone stayed at zoom 12.6, the constituency level), and it was reported as the person's move. It now runs only when the padding changes, marked as the app's. The canvas also reports its level once on load, since the screen can't know a fitted zoom in advance.
- **The hint sits with the colour switches, and the legend under them.** At street level MapLibre's credits wrap to two lines along the bottom of the map, under where the legend and hint were.
- **The panel keeps "Raised at the door"** from today's Doors view (the area's top five issues, `doorIssues`), the one part of it the map doesn't replace.

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `plusCodeCentre`, `PLUS_CODE` and `wardAt`, `wardShapes` (Task 2); `mapAreaFor`, `MapArea`, `HouseCounts` (Task 3); `getAreaDoors`, `getWardHouses`, `getMapConfig` with `MapConfig.mapArea`, and from `houses.ts` `houseColour`, `houseLegend`, `wardLegend`, `wardShade`, `doorsFirst`, `doorTotals`, `NO_DATA`, `Colour`, `LegendItem`, `WardDoors` (Task 6); `cameraFor`, `hintFor`, `Basemap`, `CameraTarget`, `Level` (Task 7); `VotersCanvas`, `WardsGeo` (Task 10). Unchanged: `getVoters`, `getPeople`, `getCanvassing`, `getAtlas`, `wardKey`, `figuresFor`, `shadeOf`, `shadeLegend`, `defaultRace`, `getJson`, `LastTimeHere`, `PeopleView`, `ManageRecords`, `AddPerson`.
- Produces:
  - In `voters-view.ts`: `VotersSearch` gains `tab?: "people"`, `house?: string` (an upper-cased plus code), `colour?: Colour` and `base?: Basemap`, and loses `view`; `type Home = { name: string; top: Top }`; `areaTitle(a, wards, home = Nairobi)` and `crumbsOf(a, wards, home = Nairobi)`; `peopleForward` returns `{ tab: "people", person? }`; `stepFor` makes a new area, tab or house a step; `VotersView`, `viewsFor`, `viewOf` and `walkNote` are gone.
  - `MapPanel({ area, wards, canvass, doors, houses, level, isAgent, onArea, onSearch })`.
  - The screen's address: `?area=w:<slug>|c:<name>&house=<plus code>&colour=support|vote&base=streets&tab=people`. Task 12 adds the house card and the search box to this screen; Task 13 the sheet.

How the screen works:
- **Map tab:** `.vscreen` is fixed under the app's header (`inset: 0 0 0 256px`, or `64px 0 0 0` below 1,100 px, where the sticky top bar is 64 px), so the page doesn't scroll and the wheel always goes to the map. `.vbar` holds the crumbs and title, and the Map/People tabs. `.vbody` is a grid of the map and the `.vpanel` (`clamp(340px, 30vw, 440px)`, scrolling on its own). Until Task 13, the panel sits under the map below 1,100 px.
- **People tab:** a normal page: the bar, the actions (Message these people, People list · CSV, Add a person, Manage records) and `PeopleView`. Agents get the map only, with no tabs.
- **No map area:** the map's place says "No map for this campaign yet: its area's houses aren't loaded. The People tab works as before." (agents: the first sentence only), and the panel still shows the numbers (Review Focus 1).
- **Data:** `getVoters`, `getPeople` (not for agents), `getCanvassing`, `getMapConfig`, `getAreaDoors` (on the map tab), the area's ward shapes and house counts (`getJson`), `getWardHouses` for the ward on show unless the colour is the 2022 vote, and `getAtlas` only for that colour. Before the voters-map migration, doors read as null: wards are grey, the legend says "Door counts not in yet" and the panel says the house data isn't in yet.
- **Colours:** wards by `wardShade` (Doors, Support) or, for the 2022 vote, today's `shadeOf(figuresFor(atlas, wardKey(atlas, constituency, null), race, 2022), "lean")`, else `NO_DATA`; houses by `houseColour` over the ward's latest visits. The legend is `houseLegend` at street level, else `wardLegend`, or `shadeLegend("lean")` plus "Not found yet" for the vote.
- **Camera and address:** the camera follows the address. A new area, or a house opened from outside the map (a link, Forward), is flown to with `cameraFor`. What the map writes itself is marked in `fromMap` and isn't flown again: the person's moves replace the step with the area under the centre (and drop the house when they leave street level), and a click on a house is a step (Back closes it) that makes the house's ward the one on show. Closing a house leaves the camera where it is.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test**

Replace `tests/voters-view.test.ts` with:

```ts
// Checks for the Voters screen's rules: the address's area, tab, house and
// filters, an area's numbers, who matches, and the links in and out. Pure;
// nothing leaves this process. Run from the repository root:
//   npx tsx --tsconfig tsconfig.json tests/voters-view.test.ts

import {
  areaNumbers,
  areaParam,
  areaTitle,
  broadcastSearch,
  crumbsOf,
  doorIssues,
  inArea,
  matchPerson,
  nextSearch,
  parseArea,
  parseBroadcastSearch,
  peopleForward,
  share,
  stepFor,
  pageWithin,
  validateVotersSearch,
  wardsIn,
  type VotersSearch,
  type WardInfo,
} from "@/lib/voters-view";

let pass = 0;
let fail = 0;

function eq(name: string, got: unknown, want: unknown) {
  const g = JSON.stringify(got);
  const w = JSON.stringify(want);
  if (g === w) pass++;
  else {
    fail++;
    console.log(`FAIL ${name}\n  got  ${g}\n  want ${w}`);
  }
}

const ward = (
  id: string,
  slug: string,
  name: string,
  constituency: string,
  n: number,
): WardInfo => ({
  id,
  slug,
  name,
  constituency,
  registered: n * 1000,
  target: n * 100,
  supporters: n * 40,
  people: n * 50,
  contacted: n * 5,
});
const WARDS = [
  ward("w1", "kileleshwa", "Kileleshwa", "Dagoretti North", 2),
  ward("w2", "kawangware", "Kawangware", "Dagoretti North", 3),
  ward("w3", "mabatini", "Mabatini", "Mathare", 1),
];

eq(
  "an address, cleaned",
  validateVotersSearch({
    area: "w:kileleshwa",
    tab: "people",
    house: " 6gcrpqm6+v9f4 ",
    colour: "support",
    base: "streets",
    support: "strong",
    consent: "sms",
    contact: "week",
    person: "p-1",
    manage: true,
    other: "x",
  }),
  {
    area: "w:kileleshwa",
    tab: "people",
    house: "6GCRPQM6+V9F4",
    colour: "support",
    base: "streets",
    support: "strong",
    consent: "sms",
    contact: "week",
    person: "p-1",
    manage: true,
  },
);
eq(
  "unknown values are dropped",
  validateVotersSearch({ tab: "doors", colour: "red", base: "moon", support: "max" }),
  {},
);
eq(
  "a bad house is dropped, so the map opens the area instead",
  [
    validateVotersSearch({ house: "hello" }),
    validateVotersSearch({ house: "6GCRPQM6+V9F4;x" }),
    validateVotersSearch({ house: 42 }),
  ],
  [{}, {}, {}],
);
eq("manage from a link", validateVotersSearch({ manage: 1 }).manage, true);
eq(
  "old links: view=people is the People tab; view=doors and view=wards open the map",
  [
    validateVotersSearch({ view: "people", person: "p-9" }),
    validateVotersSearch({ view: "doors" }),
    validateVotersSearch({ view: "wards", area: "c:Westlands" }),
  ],
  [{ tab: "people", person: "p-9" }, {}, { area: "c:Westlands" }],
);

eq("the county", parseArea(undefined, WARDS), { level: "county" });
eq("a constituency", parseArea("c:Dagoretti North", WARDS), {
  level: "constituency",
  name: "Dagoretti North",
});
eq("a ward", parseArea("w:mabatini", WARDS), { level: "ward", slug: "mabatini" });
eq("an unknown ward is the county", parseArea("w:nowhere", WARDS), { level: "county" });
eq("an unknown constituency too", parseArea("c:Atlantis", WARDS), { level: "county" });
eq(
  "back to the address",
  [
    areaParam({ level: "county" }),
    areaParam({ level: "constituency", name: "Mathare" }),
    areaParam({ level: "ward", slug: "mabatini" }),
  ],
  [undefined, "c:Mathare", "w:mabatini"],
);

const DN = { level: "constituency", name: "Dagoretti North" } as const;
const KILE = { level: "ward", slug: "kileleshwa" } as const;
eq(
  "the wards in a constituency",
  wardsIn(DN, WARDS).map((w) => w.id),
  ["w1", "w2"],
);
eq(
  "titles",
  [areaTitle({ level: "county" }, WARDS), areaTitle(DN, WARDS), areaTitle(KILE, WARDS)],
  ["Nairobi County", "Dagoretti North", "Kileleshwa ward"],
);
eq(
  "the breadcrumb to a ward",
  crumbsOf(KILE, WARDS).map((c) => [c.label, areaParam(c.area) ?? null]),
  [
    ["Nairobi County", null],
    ["Dagoretti North", "c:Dagoretti North"],
    ["Kileleshwa ward", "w:kileleshwa"],
  ],
);
{
  const MATHIRA = { name: "Mathira", top: "constituency" } as const;
  const wards = [ward("m1", "konyu", "Konyu", "Mathira", 2)];
  eq(
    "a constituency's map: its name on top, and no crumb for itself",
    [
      areaTitle({ level: "county" }, wards, MATHIRA),
      crumbsOf({ level: "ward", slug: "konyu" }, wards, MATHIRA).map((c) => [
        c.label,
        areaParam(c.area) ?? null,
      ]),
    ],
    [
      "Mathira",
      [
        ["Mathira", null],
        ["Konyu ward", "w:konyu"],
      ],
    ],
  );
}

const DOORS = [
  { id: "w1", people: 100, knocked: 40, doors30: 55 },
  { id: "w2", people: 150, knocked: 60, doors30: 70 },
];
eq("a constituency's numbers", areaNumbers(DN, WARDS, DOORS), {
  registered: 5000,
  target: 500,
  onFile: 250,
  supporters: 200,
  doors: 125,
  coverage: 0.4,
});
eq("a ward nobody has knocked", areaNumbers({ level: "ward", slug: "mabatini" }, WARDS, DOORS), {
  registered: 1000,
  target: 100,
  onFile: 50,
  supporters: 40,
  doors: 0,
  coverage: 0,
});

const NOW = Date.UTC(2026, 9, 4, 9);
const daysAgo = (d: number) => new Date(NOW - d * 864e5).toISOString();
const person = (over: Record<string, unknown> = {}) => ({
  ward: "Kileleshwa",
  constituency: "Dagoretti North",
  support: 75,
  channels: ["SMS"],
  optedOut: false,
  lastTouch: daysAgo(3),
  ...over,
});
eq(
  "in the area",
  [
    inArea("Kileleshwa", DN, WARDS),
    inArea("Mabatini", DN, WARDS),
    inArea(null, { level: "county" }, WARDS),
  ],
  [true, false, true],
);
eq(
  "a strong supporter, by SMS, seen this week",
  matchPerson(person(), DN, WARDS, { support: "strong", consent: "sms", contact: "week" }, NOW),
  true,
);
eq(
  "filters that don't match",
  [
    matchPerson(person({ support: 50 }), DN, WARDS, { support: "strong" }, NOW),
    matchPerson(person({ support: 50 }), DN, WARDS, { support: "persuadable" }, NOW),
    matchPerson(person({ support: 0 }), DN, WARDS, { support: "unscored" }, NOW),
    matchPerson(person({ support: 20 }), DN, WARDS, { support: "against" }, NOW),
    matchPerson(person({ optedOut: true }), DN, WARDS, { consent: "sms" }, NOW),
    matchPerson(person({ channels: [] }), DN, WARDS, { consent: "none" }, NOW),
    matchPerson(person({ lastTouch: daysAgo(20) }), DN, WARDS, { contact: "week" }, NOW),
    matchPerson(person({ lastTouch: daysAgo(20) }), DN, WARDS, { contact: "month" }, NOW),
    matchPerson(person({ lastTouch: null }), DN, WARDS, { contact: "never" }, NOW),
    matchPerson(person({ ward: "Mabatini", constituency: "Mathare" }), DN, WARDS, {}, NOW),
  ],
  [false, true, true, true, false, true, false, true, true, false],
);

eq("a constituency carries all its wards", broadcastSearch(DN, WARDS, "strong"), {
  wards: "w1,w2",
  support: "strong",
});
eq("the county, everyone", broadcastSearch({ level: "county" }, WARDS), {});
eq("Broadcast reads it back", parseBroadcastSearch({ wards: "w1,w2", support: "persuadable" }), {
  wardIds: ["w1", "w2"],
  strong: false,
  persuadable: true,
});
eq("Broadcast with nothing chosen keeps its defaults", parseBroadcastSearch({}), {
  wardIds: [],
  strong: true,
  persuadable: true,
});
eq("an old People link keeps its person", peopleForward({ person: "p-9" }), {
  tab: "people",
  person: "p-9",
});
eq("an old People link without one", peopleForward({}), { tab: "people" });

eq(
  "a change to the address; undefined removes",
  nextSearch(
    { area: "w:kileleshwa", tab: "people", person: "p-1" },
    { area: "c:Mathare", person: undefined },
  ),
  { area: "c:Mathare", tab: "people" },
);
eq(
  "an old view=people still in the address doesn't hold the People tab open",
  nextSearch({ view: "people", area: "c:Mathare" } as VotersSearch, { tab: undefined }),
  { area: "c:Mathare" },
);
eq(
  "what came up at the door across an area",
  doorIssues(
    [
      {
        name: "Kileleshwa",
        issues: [
          { name: "Water", count: 3 },
          { name: "Roads", count: 1 },
        ],
      },
      {
        name: "Kawangware",
        issues: [
          { name: "Water", count: 2 },
          { name: "Jobs", count: 2 },
        ],
      },
      { name: "Mabatini", issues: [{ name: "Roads", count: 9 }] },
    ],
    DN,
    WARDS,
  ),
  [
    { name: "Water", count: 5 },
    { name: "Jobs", count: 2 },
    { name: "Roads", count: 1 },
  ],
);

eq(
  "a share too small to round is not 0%",
  [share(0.0002), share(0), share(0.336), share(1)],
  ["under 1%", "0%", "34%", "100%"],
);

eq(
  "a new area, tab or house is a step Back undoes; only a new area or tab goes back to the top",
  [
    stepFor({ area: "c:Mathare", house: undefined }),
    stepFor({ tab: undefined }),
    stepFor({ house: "6GCRPQM6+V9F4" }),
    stepFor({ colour: "support" }),
    stepFor({ support: "strong" }),
  ],
  [
    { replace: false, resetScroll: true },
    { replace: false, resetScroll: true },
    { replace: false, resetScroll: false },
    { replace: true, resetScroll: false },
    { replace: true, resetScroll: false },
  ],
);

eq(
  "a page past the end of a shorter list comes back to the last page",
  [pageWithin(3, 5, 12), pageWithin(1, 30, 12), pageWithin(4, 30, 12), pageWithin(2, 0, 12)],
  [0, 1, 2, 0],
);
eq(
  "the county counts people not yet placed in a ward",
  areaNumbers({ level: "county" }, WARDS, DOORS, { people: 50, knocked: 10, doors30: 7 }),
  { registered: 6000, target: 600, onFile: 350, supporters: 240, doors: 132, coverage: 110 / 300 },
);
eq(
  "a constituency doesn't",
  areaNumbers(DN, WARDS, DOORS, { people: 50, knocked: 10, doors30: 7 }).onFile,
  250,
);
eq("a group in the address", validateVotersSearch({ segment: "boda" }).segment, "boda");
eq("a group that isn't a slug is dropped", validateVotersSearch({ segment: "Boda Riders!" }), {});
eq(
  "the group filter",
  [
    matchPerson(person({ segment: "boda" }), DN, WARDS, { segment: "boda" }, NOW),
    matchPerson(person({ segment: "youth" }), DN, WARDS, { segment: "boda" }, NOW),
    matchPerson(person({ segment: null }), DN, WARDS, { segment: "boda" }, NOW),
  ],
  [true, false, false],
);

console.log(`${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);
process.exit(fail ? 1 : 0);
```

```diff
diff --git a/src/styles/groundwork.css b/src/styles/groundwork.css
--- a/src/styles/groundwork.css
+++ b/src/styles/groundwork.css
@@ -2065,6 +2065,47 @@ figure { margin: 0; }
   .vt-actions { justify-content:flex-start; }
 }
 
+/* Voters: the map owns the screen below the app's header, with the crumbs and tabs above
+   it and the panel beside it (a sheet on phones); the People tab is a page. */
+.vscreen { position:fixed; inset:0 0 0 256px; z-index:20; display:flex; flex-direction:column; background:var(--gw-paper); }
+.vbar { display:flex; align-items:center; gap:10px 16px; flex-wrap:wrap; }
+.vscreen .vbar { padding:12px 20px; border-bottom:1px solid var(--border); background:var(--card); }
+.vbar-place { display:flex; align-items:baseline; gap:8px; flex-wrap:wrap; min-width:0; }
+.vbar h1 { font-size:22px; font-weight:600; line-height:1.2; }
+.vbar .spacer { flex:1; }
+.vbody { flex:1; min-height:0; display:grid; grid-template-columns:minmax(0,1fr) clamp(340px, 30vw, 440px); }
+.vbody .vmap { height:100%; border:0; border-radius:0; }
+.vmap--none { background:var(--muted); }
+.vmap--none .vmap-loading { color:var(--muted-foreground); padding:24px; text-align:center; }
+.vpanel { min-height:0; overflow-y:auto; overscroll-behavior:contain; border-left:1px solid var(--border); padding:16px 18px 28px; display:flex; flex-direction:column; gap:18px; }
+/* A scrolling column: its parts keep their height (the ladder clips, so it would otherwise shrink to nothing). */
+.vpanel > * { flex-shrink:0; }
+.vpanel h2 { font-size:15px; font-weight:600; }
+.vpanel .card-head { margin-bottom:6px; }
+.vpanel .ladder { grid-template-columns:repeat(2, minmax(0, 1fr)); }
+.vpanel .ladder > * { border-top:1px solid var(--border); }
+.vpanel .ladder > :nth-child(odd) { border-left:0; }
+.vpanel .ladder > :nth-child(-n+2) { border-top:0; }
+.vpanel .ladder > :last-child:nth-child(odd) { grid-column:1 / -1; }
+.vnext { display:flex; flex-direction:column; gap:8px; }
+.vnext-row { display:flex; align-items:center; justify-content:space-between; gap:10px; padding:10px 12px; border:1px solid var(--border); border-radius:12px; background:var(--card); }
+.vnext-what { display:flex; flex-direction:column; gap:2px; min-width:0; }
+.vnext-what .meta { font-size:12px; }
+.vnext-row .btn { flex:none; white-space:nowrap; }
+.vtag { align-self:flex-start; font-size:9.5px; letter-spacing:.14em; text-transform:uppercase; font-weight:600; padding:2px 7px; border-radius:999px; background:hsl(15 77% 52% / .12); color:hsl(15 70% 38%); }
+/* The switches, the hint and the legend sit at the top, clear of the credits along the bottom. */
+.vbody .vtop { flex-direction:column; align-items:flex-start; }
+.vtop-row { display:flex; gap:8px; flex-wrap:wrap; align-items:center; pointer-events:none; }
+.vtop-row > * { pointer-events:auto; }
+.vtop .vhint { position:static; transform:none; white-space:normal; }
+.vtop .vleg { position:static; max-width:100%; }
+.vt-actions--page { display:flex; justify-content:flex-start; gap:8px; margin-top:14px; }
+@media (max-width:1100px) {
+  .vscreen { inset:64px 0 0 0; }
+  .vbody { grid-template-columns:1fr; grid-template-rows:minmax(0, 1fr) minmax(0, 40%); }
+  .vpanel { border-left:0; border-top:1px solid var(--border); }
+}
+
 /* Elections: the switches, what to do, the results, the map, notes, and the printed brief */
 .el-switches { display:flex; flex-wrap:wrap; gap:10px; margin:6px 0 16px; }
 .el-actions { flex-direction:row; flex-wrap:wrap; justify-content:flex-end; align-items:center; }
```
