- [ ] **Step 4: Write `src/components/gw/voters/MapPanel.tsx`**

```tsx
// The panel beside the Voters map (a sheet on phones): the area's five numbers
// and what happened here last time, then its doors (houses knocked, support
// found, doors this week, agents out today) and "Do this next", the wards with
// the most houses not knocked in 30 days. In a ward, the way down to its houses.

import { LastTimeHere } from "@/components/gw/voters/LastTimeHere";
import type { CanvassData } from "@/lib/console.functions";
import { doorsFirst, doorTotals, type WardDoors } from "@/lib/houses";
import type { Level } from "@/lib/voters-map";
import {
  areaNumbers,
  doorIssues,
  share,
  wardsIn,
  type Area,
  type SearchChanges,
  type WardInfo,
} from "@/lib/voters-view";

const nf = new Intl.NumberFormat("en-KE");

type Figure = { label: string; value: string; note: string; open?: () => void };

export function MapPanel({
  area,
  wards,
  canvass,
  doors,
  houses,
  level,
  isAgent,
  onArea,
  onSearch,
}: {
  area: Area;
  wards: WardInfo[];
  canvass: CanvassData | null;
  /** Doors per ward: undefined while they're read, null when they can't be (the update isn't in). */
  doors: WardDoors[] | null | undefined;
  /** Houses on the map per ward slug; empty without a house file. */
  houses: Record<string, number>;
  /** The map's level: at street level the houses are on show. */
  level: Level;
  isAgent: boolean;
  onArea: (a: Area) => void;
  /** Opens the People tab with its filters; agents don't get it. */
  onSearch: (changes: SearchChanges) => void;
}) {
  const inside = wardsIn(area, wards);
  const n = areaNumbers(area, wards, canvass?.wards ?? [], canvass?.unplaced);
  const figures: Figure[] = [
    {
      label: "Registered voters",
      value: nf.format(n.registered),
      note: `${inside.length} ${inside.length === 1 ? "ward" : "wards"} on the register`,
    },
    {
      label: "People on file",
      value: nf.format(n.onFile),
      note: n.registered
        ? `${share(n.onFile / n.registered)} of the register`
        : "on the campaign's records",
      ...(isAgent
        ? {}
        : {
            open: () =>
              onSearch({
                tab: "people",
                support: undefined,
                consent: undefined,
                contact: undefined,
              }),
          }),
    },
    {
      label: "Supporters found",
      value: nf.format(n.supporters),
      note: n.target
        ? `${share(n.supporters / n.target)} of the ${nf.format(n.target)} needed`
        : "no target set",
      ...(isAgent ? {} : { open: () => onSearch({ tab: "people", support: "strong" }) }),
    },
    {
      label: "Doors · 30 days",
      value: canvass ? nf.format(n.doors) : "—",
      note: "knocked, answered or not",
    },
    {
      label: "Coverage",
      value: canvass && n.coverage !== null ? share(n.coverage) : "—",
      note: "of people on file seen in 30 days",
    },
  ];
  const t = doors ? doorTotals(inside, houses, doors) : null;
  const next = doors && area.level !== "ward" ? doorsFirst(inside, houses, doors) : [];
  const issues = canvass ? doorIssues(canvass.wards, area, wards, 5) : [];

  return (
    <>
      <div className="ladder vt-numbers">
        {figures.map((f) => {
          const inner = (
            <>
              <span className="l">{f.label}</span>
              <span className="n stat">{f.value}</span>
              <span className="s">{f.note}</span>
            </>
          );
          return f.open ? (
            <button key={f.label} type="button" onClick={f.open}>
              {inner}
            </button>
          ) : (
            <div key={f.label}>{inner}</div>
          );
        })}
      </div>

      {isAgent ? null : <LastTimeHere area={area} wards={wards} />}

      <section aria-label="Doors">
        <div className="card-head">
          <h2>Doors</h2>
          <span className="mono">houses on the map · 30 days</span>
        </div>
        {doors === undefined ? (
          <p className="meta">Reading the doors…</p>
        ) : t === null ? (
          <p className="meta">
            The house data isn't in yet: houses knocked are counted once the database update is
            applied.
          </p>
        ) : (
          <div className="f-rows">
            <div className="f-row">
              <span>Houses knocked</span>
              <b>
                {t.houses
                  ? `${nf.format(t.knocked)} of ${nf.format(t.houses)} · ${share(t.knocked / t.houses)}`
                  : nf.format(t.knocked)}
              </b>
            </div>
            <div className="f-row">
              <span>Supporters among doors scored</span>
              <b>
                {t.rated
                  ? `${nf.format(t.supporters)} of ${nf.format(t.rated)}`
                  : "none scored yet"}
              </b>
            </div>
            <div className="f-row">
              <span>Doors this week</span>
              <b>{nf.format(t.doorsWeek)}</b>
            </div>
            <div className="f-row">
              <span>Agents out today</span>
              <b>{nf.format(t.agentsToday)}</b>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}
        {area.level === "ward" && level !== "street" ? (
          <p className="f-note">Zoom in to street level to see each house.</p>
        ) : null}
      </section>

      {area.level !== "ward" && t ? (
        <section className="vnext" aria-label="Do this next">
          <div className="card-head">
            <h2>Do this next</h2>
            <span className="mono">most houses not knocked</span>
          </div>
          {next.length ? (
            next.map((r) => (
              <div className="vnext-row" key={r.wardId}>
                <div className="vnext-what">
                  <span className="vtag">Doors first</span>
                  <b>{r.name} ward</b>
                  <span className="meta">
                    {nf.format(r.notKnocked)} of {nf.format(r.houses)} houses not knocked in 30 days
                  </span>
                </div>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  className="btn btn--ghost btn--sm"
                  onClick={() => onArea({ level: "ward", slug: r.slug })}
                >
                  Open the ward
                </button>
              </div>
            ))
          ) : (
            <p className="meta">No houses on the map for these wards yet.</p>
          )}
        </section>
      ) : null}

      {issues.length ? (
        <section aria-label="Raised at the door">
          <div className="card-head">
            <h2>Raised at the door</h2>
            <span className="mono">from conversations</span>
          </div>
          <div className="f-rows">
            {issues.map((i) => (
              <div className="f-row" key={i.name}>
                <span>{i.name}</span>
                <b>{nf.format(i.count)}</b>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        </section>
      ) : null}
    </>
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Rewrite `src/routes/_authenticated/voters.tsx`, and remove the old screen's parts**

Replace `src/routes/_authenticated/voters.tsx` with:

```tsx
// Voters: the campaign's map, from its whole area down to one house, with a
// panel of numbers and next steps beside it (a sheet on phones), and a People
// tab with the list for the area on show. The address holds the area, the
// house, the colour, the base map and the tab, so a link or a reload opens the
// same view. A click, a crumb, a link, Back or Forward flies the map there; a
// move the person makes on the map writes the address and isn't flown again.

import { createFileRoute, Link, useNavigate } from "@tanstack/react-router";
import { useQuery, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { useServerFn } from "@tanstack/react-start";
import { Fragment, useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState, type ReactNode } from "react";

import { AddPerson } from "@/components/gw/AddPerson";
import { ManageRecords } from "@/components/gw/voters/ManageRecords";
import { MapPanel } from "@/components/gw/voters/MapPanel";
import { PeopleView } from "@/components/gw/voters/PeopleView";
import { VotersCanvas, type WardsGeo } from "@/components/gw/voters/VotersCanvas";
import { useAccess } from "@/hooks/useAccess";
import { wardKey } from "@/lib/atlas-app";
import { getAtlas } from "@/lib/atlas.functions";
import { figuresFor, shadeLegend, shadeOf } from "@/lib/atlas-view";
import { getCanvassing, getPeople, getVoters, type CanvassData } from "@/lib/console.functions";
import { downloadCSV, stampName } from "@/lib/csv";
import { defaultRace } from "@/lib/elections-view";
import { wardAt, wardShapes } from "@/lib/geo";
import { getJson } from "@/lib/geo-files";
import {
  houseColour,
  houseLegend,
  NO_DATA,
  wardLegend,
  wardShade,
  type Colour,
  type LegendItem,
  type WardDoors,
} from "@/lib/houses";
import { getAreaDoors, getWardHouses } from "@/lib/houses.functions";
import { getMapConfig } from "@/lib/map.functions";
import { mapAreaFor, type HouseCounts, type MapArea } from "@/lib/map-areas";
import { plusCodeCentre } from "@/lib/pluscode";
import { cameraFor, hintFor, type Basemap, type CameraTarget, type Level } from "@/lib/voters-map";
import {
  areaParam,
  areaTitle,
  broadcastSearch,
  crumbsOf,
  inArea,
  matchPerson,
  nextSearch,
  parseArea,
  stepFor,
  validateVotersSearch,
  type Area,
  type Home,
  type SearchChanges,
  type VotersSearch,
  type WardInfo,
} from "@/lib/voters-view";

const ABOUT =
  "Pick an area, from the county down to one house: its people, its doors and its numbers, on one map.";

export const Route = createFileRoute("/_authenticated/voters")({
  component: Voters,
  // ?area=w:kileleshwa&house=6GCRPQM6+V9F4&colour=support: the view on show, so a link opens it.
  validateSearch: validateVotersSearch,
  head: () => ({
    meta: [
      { title: "Voters · Groundwork" },
      { name: "description", content: ABOUT },
      { property: "og:title", content: "Voters · Groundwork" },
      { property: "og:description", content: ABOUT },
      { property: "og:type", content: "website" },
      { name: "twitter:card", content: "summary_large_image" },
    ],
  }),
});

/** A campaign with no map area: the People tab's top. */
const NO_MAP: Home = { name: "Every ward", top: "county" };
const COLOURS: { key: Colour; label: string }[] = [
  { key: "doors", label: "Doors" },
  { key: "support", label: "Support" },
  { key: "vote", label: "2022 vote" },
];

/** The area's people to see next, as a CSV for a canvasser. */
function exportPeople(canvass: CanvassData | null, area: Area, wards: WardInfo[], place: string) {
  const list = (canvass?.walkList ?? []).filter((p) => inArea(p.ward, area, wards));
  downloadCSV(
    stampName(`groundwork-people-list-${place.toLowerCase().replace(/\s+/g, "-")}`),
    [
      "Name",
      "Phone",
      "Ward",
      "Group",
      "Support score",
      "Last contacted",
      "Why they are on the list",
    ],
    list.map((p) => [
      p.name,
      p.phone,
      p.ward ?? "",
      p.segment ?? "",
      p.support,
      p.lastTouch ? p.lastTouch.slice(0, 10) : "never",
      p.reason,
    ]),
  );
}

function Voters() {
  const search = Route.useSearch();
  const navigate = useNavigate({ from: Route.fullPath });
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  const { access, isPrincipal } = useAccess();
  const isAgent = access?.role === "agent";
  const fetchVoters = useServerFn(getVoters);
  const fetchPeople = useServerFn(getPeople);
  const fetchCanvassing = useServerFn(getCanvassing);
  const fetchConfig = useServerFn(getMapConfig);
  const fetchDoors = useServerFn(getAreaDoors);
  const tab = search.tab === "people" && !isAgent ? "people" : "map";
  const { data } = useQuery({ queryKey: ["voters"], queryFn: () => fetchVoters() });
  // The People list isn't on an agent's screen, so it isn't fetched for them.
  const { data: people } = useQuery({
    queryKey: ["people"],
    queryFn: () => fetchPeople(),
    enabled: Boolean(access) && !isAgent,
  });
  const { data: canvass } = useQuery({
    queryKey: ["canvassing"],
    queryFn: () => fetchCanvassing(),
  });
  const { data: config, isError: configFailed } = useQuery({
    queryKey: ["map-config"],
    queryFn: () => fetchConfig(),
    staleTime: Infinity,
  });
  const mapArea = mapAreaFor(config?.mapArea);
  const { data: doors } = useQuery({
    queryKey: ["area-doors"],
    queryFn: () => fetchDoors(),
    enabled: tab === "map",
  });
  const { data: counts } = useQuery({
    queryKey: ["geo", mapArea?.id, "counts"],
    queryFn: () => getJson<HouseCounts>(mapArea!.counts),
    enabled: Boolean(mapArea),
    staleTime: Infinity,
  });
  const [adding, setAdding] = useState(false);
  const [level, setLevel] = useState<Level>("area");

  const wards = useMemo(() => data?.wards ?? [], [data]);
  const rows = useMemo(() => {
    const a = parseArea(search.area, wards);
    const now = Date.now();
    return (people?.rows ?? []).filter((r) => matchPerson(r, a, wards, search, now));
  }, [people, wards, search]);

  const go = (changes: SearchChanges) =>
    void navigate({ search: (prev) => nextSearch(prev, changes), ...stepFor(changes) });
  const setArea = (a: Area) => go({ area: areaParam(a), house: undefined, person: undefined });
  const home: Home = mapArea ? { name: mapArea.name, top: mapArea.top } : NO_MAP;

  if (!data || (!config && !configFailed)) {
    return (
      <section className="view active" aria-label="Voters">
        <div className="vh">
          <div>
            <span className="eyebrow">Voters · county to doorstep</span>
            <h1>{home.name}</h1>
            <p className="meta">Loading the wards…</p>
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>
    );
  }

  const area = parseArea(search.area, wards);
  const title = areaTitle(area, wards, home);
  const crumbs = crumbsOf(area, wards, home);
  const record = search.person ? (people?.rows.find((r) => r.id === search.person) ?? null) : null;
  const bar = (
    <Bar
      title={title}
      crumbs={crumbs}
      onArea={setArea}
      {...(isAgent
        ? {}
        : {
            tab,
            onTab: (t: "map" | "people") => go({ tab: t === "people" ? "people" : undefined }),
          })}
    />
  );
  const modals = (
    <>
      {adding && people && (
        <AddPerson
          wards={people.wards}
          segments={people.segments}
          onClose={() => {
            setAdding(false);
            void queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["voters"] });
          }}
        />
      )}
      {search.manage && !isAgent && people && (
        <ManageRecords
          data={people}
          canImport={isPrincipal}
          onClose={() => go({ manage: undefined })}
        />
      )}
    </>
  );

  if (tab === "people") {
    return (
      <section className="view active" aria-label="Voters">
        {modals}
        {bar}
        <div className="vt-actions vt-actions--page fx">
          <Link
            to="/broadcast"
            search={broadcastSearch(area, wards, search.support)}
            className="btn btn--primary btn--sm"
          >
            Message these people
          </Link>
          <button
            type="button"
            className="btn btn--ghost btn--sm"
            disabled={!canvass}
            onClick={() => exportPeople(canvass ?? null, area, wards, title)}
          >
            People list · CSV
          </button>
          <button
            type="button"
            className="btn btn--ghost btn--sm"
            disabled={!people}
            onClick={() => setAdding(true)}
          >
            Add a person
          </button>
          <button
            type="button"
            className="btn btn--ghost btn--sm"
            onClick={() => go({ manage: true })}
          >
            Manage records
          </button>
        </div>
        <div className="vt-panel fx2">
          <PeopleView
            rows={rows}
            record={record}
            loading={!people}
            search={search}
            onSearch={go}
            placeName={title}
            segments={people?.segments ?? []}
          />
        </div>
      </section>
    );
  }

  const panel = (
    <MapPanel
      area={area}
      wards={wards}
      canvass={canvass ?? null}
      doors={doors}
      houses={counts?.wards ?? {}}
      level={level}
      isAgent={isAgent}
      onArea={setArea}
      onSearch={go}
    />
  );
  return (
    <section className="view active" aria-label="Voters">
      {modals}
      <div className="vscreen">
        {bar}
        {mapArea ? (
          <MapScreen
            mapArea={mapArea}
            maptilerKey={config?.maptilerKey ?? null}
            wards={wards}
            area={area}
            search={search}
            doors={doors}
            counts={counts ?? null}
            level={level}
            onLevel={setLevel}
            onGo={go}
            onMapMove={(changes) =>
              void navigate({ search: (prev) => nextSearch(prev, changes), replace: true })
            }
            panel={panel}
          />
        ) : (
          <div className="vbody">
            <div className="vmap vmap--none">
              <p className="vmap-loading meta">
                {configFailed
                  ? "Could not load the map's settings. Try reloading."
                  : isAgent
                    ? "No map for this campaign yet: its area's houses aren't loaded."
                    : "No map for this campaign yet: its area's houses aren't loaded. The People tab works as before."}
              </p>
            </div>
            <aside className="vpanel" aria-label="This area">
              {panel}
            </aside>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}

/** The crumbs to the area on show, and the Map and People tabs. */
function Bar({
  title,
  crumbs,
  onArea,
  tab,
  onTab,
  children,
}: {
  title: string;
  crumbs: { label: string; area: Area }[];
  onArea: (a: Area) => void;
  /** Left out for agents, who get the map only. */
  tab?: "map" | "people";
  onTab?: (t: "map" | "people") => void;
  children?: ReactNode;
}) {
  return (
    <div className="vbar">
      <div className="vbar-place">
        {crumbs.length > 1 ? (
          <nav className="crumbs" aria-label="Area">
            {crumbs.slice(0, -1).map((c) => (
              <Fragment key={c.label}>
                <button type="button" onClick={() => onArea(c.area)}>
                  {c.label}
                </button>
                <span className="sep">/</span>
              </Fragment>
            ))}
          </nav>
        ) : null}
        <h1>{title}</h1>
      </div>
      <span className="spacer" />
      {children}
      {tab && onTab ? (
        <div className="seg" role="group" aria-label="Show">
          <button type="button" aria-pressed={tab === "map"} onClick={() => onTab("map")}>
            Map
          </button>
          <button type="button" aria-pressed={tab === "people"} onClick={() => onTab("people")}>
            People
          </button>
        </div>
      ) : null}
    </div>
  );
}

const addressKey = (s: { area?: string; house?: string }) => `${s.area ?? ""}|${s.house ?? ""}`;

/** The map and its controls beside the panel. */
function MapScreen({
  mapArea,
  maptilerKey,
  wards,
  area,
  search,
  doors,
  counts,
  level,
  onLevel,
  onGo,
  onMapMove,
  panel,
}: {
  mapArea: MapArea;
  maptilerKey: string | null;
  wards: WardInfo[];
  area: Area;
  search: VotersSearch;
  doors: WardDoors[] | null | undefined;
  counts: HouseCounts | null;
  level: Level;
  onLevel: (level: Level) => void;
  /** A change to the address, a step or not as stepFor says; a new area is flown to. */
  onGo: (changes: SearchChanges) => void;
  /** The person moved the map: the address follows, replacing the step. */
  onMapMove: (changes: SearchChanges) => void;
  panel: ReactNode;
}) {
  const { campaign } = useAccess();
  const fetchHouses = useServerFn(getWardHouses);
  const fetchAtlas = useServerFn(getAtlas);
  const [tilesRefused, setTilesRefused] = useState(false);
  const colour: Colour = search.colour ?? "doors";
  const keyOk = Boolean(maptilerKey) && !tilesRefused;
  const base: Basemap = keyOk ? (search.base ?? "satellite") : "streets";

  const { data: geo, isError: geoFailed } = useQuery({
    queryKey: ["geo", mapArea.id, "wards"],
    queryFn: () => getJson<WardsGeo>(mapArea.wards),
    staleTime: Infinity,
  });
  const shapes = useMemo(() => (geo ? wardShapes(geo) : []), [geo]);
  const ward = area.level === "ward" ? (wards.find((w) => w.slug === area.slug) ?? null) : null;
  const { data: houses } = useQuery({
    queryKey: ["ward-houses", ward?.id],
    queryFn: () => fetchHouses({ data: { wardId: ward!.id } }),
    enabled: Boolean(ward) && colour !== "vote",
  });
  const { data: atlas } = useQuery({
    queryKey: ["atlas"],
    queryFn: () => fetchAtlas(),
    enabled: colour === "vote",
    staleTime: 5 * 60_000,
  });

  const wardFill = useMemo(() => {
    if (colour === "vote") {
      const race = defaultRace(campaign?.level);
      return Object.fromEntries(
        wards.map((w) => {
          const key = atlas ? wardKey(atlas, w.constituency, null) : null;
          const shade = atlas && key ? shadeOf(figuresFor(atlas, key, race, 2022), "lean") : null;
          return [w.slug, shade?.colour ?? NO_DATA];
        }),
      );
    }
    const byWard = new Map((doors ?? []).map((d) => [d.wardId, d]));
    return Object.fromEntries(
      wards.map((w) => [
        w.slug,
        doors ? wardShade(colour, counts?.wards[w.slug] ?? 0, byWard.get(w.id)) : NO_DATA,
      ]),
    );
  }, [colour, atlas, wards, doors, counts, campaign?.level]);
  const houseColours = useMemo(() => {
    const out = new Map<string, string>();
    for (const h of houses ?? []) {
      const c = houseColour(colour, h);
      if (c) out.set(h.pc, c);
    }
    return out;
  }, [houses, colour]);
  const legend: LegendItem[] =
    level === "street"
      ? houseLegend(colour)
      : colour === "vote"
        ? [...shadeLegend("lean"), { label: "Not found yet", colour: NO_DATA }]
        : doors === null
          ? [{ label: "Door counts not in yet", colour: NO_DATA }]
          : wardLegend(colour);

  // The camera follows the address: a new area, or a house opened from outside
  // the map, is flown to. What the map wrote itself (fromMap) isn't, and
  // closing a house leaves the camera where it is.
  const key = addressKey(search);
  const fromMap = useRef<string | null>(null);
  const last = useRef<{ area: string | undefined; house: string | undefined } | null>(null);
  const [camera, setCamera] = useState<{ seq: number; target: CameraTarget } | null>(null);
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!geo) return;
    const prev = last.current;
    last.current = { area: search.area, house: search.house };
    const mine = fromMap.current === key;
    fromMap.current = null;
    if (mine) return;
    const areaChanged = !prev || prev.area !== search.area;
    const houseOpened = Boolean(search.house) && prev?.house !== search.house;
    if (!areaChanged && !houseOpened) return;
    const target = cameraFor({ area, house: search.house ?? null }, wards, shapes, mapArea);
    setCamera((c) => ({ seq: (c?.seq ?? 0) + 1, target }));
    // The camera follows the address and the ward shapes, not every render.
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, [key, geo]);

  /** A change the map made: kept from being flown, as a step or replacing it. */
  function fromTheMap(changes: SearchChanges, step: boolean) {
    const next = addressKey(nextSearch(search, changes));
    if (next === key) return;
    fromMap.current = next;
    if (step) onGo(changes);
    else onMapMove(changes);
  }

  return (
    <div className="vbody">
      {geo ? (
        <VotersCanvas
          mapArea={mapArea}
          maptilerKey={keyOk ? maptilerKey : null}
          basemap={base}
          wards={geo}
          shapes={shapes}
          wardInfo={wards}
          area={area}
          wardFill={wardFill}
          houseColours={houseColours}
          house={search.house ?? null}
          camera={camera}
          padding={{ bottom: 0 }}
          onMoved={(a, at) =>
            fromTheMap(
              {
                ...(a ? { area: areaParam(a) } : {}),
                ...(at === "street" ? {} : { house: undefined }),
              },
              false,
            )
          }
          onPick={(a) => onGo({ area: areaParam(a), house: undefined })}
          onHouse={(pc) => {
            if (!pc) {
              fromTheMap({ house: undefined }, false);
              return;
            }
            // A house in the next ward makes that ward the one on show.
            const at = plusCodeCentre(pc);
            const shape = at ? wardAt(at, shapes) : null;
            fromTheMap({ house: pc, ...(shape ? { area: `w:${shape.slug}` } : {}) }, true);
          }}
          onLevel={onLevel}
          onTilesRefused={() => setTilesRefused(true)}
        >
          <div className="vtop">
            <div className="vtop-row">
              {keyOk ? (
                <div className="vglass vseg vseg--map" role="group" aria-label="Base map">
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    aria-pressed={base === "satellite"}
                    onClick={() => onGo({ base: undefined })}
                  >
                    Satellite
                  </button>
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    aria-pressed={base === "streets"}
                    onClick={() => onGo({ base: "streets" })}
                  >
                    Streets
                  </button>
                </div>
              ) : null}
              <div className="vglass vseg" role="group" aria-label="Colour by">
                {COLOURS.map((c) => (
                  <button
                    key={c.key}
                    type="button"
                    aria-pressed={colour === c.key}
                    onClick={() => onGo({ colour: c.key === "doors" ? undefined : c.key })}
                  >
                    {c.label}
                  </button>
                ))}
              </div>
              <div className="vglass vhint" role="status">
                {hintFor(level, mapArea.top)}
              </div>
            </div>
            <div className="vglass vleg">
              {legend.length ? (
                legend.map((it) => (
                  <span key={it.label}>
                    <i style={{ background: it.colour }} />
                    {it.label}
                  </span>
                ))
              ) : (
                <span>Houses aren't shaded by the 2022 vote: zoom out to see the wards</span>
              )}
            </div>
          </div>
        </VotersCanvas>
      ) : (
        <div className="vmap">
          <p className="vmap-loading meta">
            {geoFailed ? "Could not load the ward boundaries. Try reloading." : "Loading the map…"}
          </p>
        </div>
      )}
      <aside className="vpanel" aria-label="This area">
        {panel}
        {tilesRefused ? (
          <p className="f-note">
            MapTiler turned the key down, so the map shows OpenFreeMap streets. Check the key and
            its allowed domains in MapTiler.
          </p>
        ) : null}
      </aside>
    </div>
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 7: See it in the harness**

With the harness running (Task 9; `$SCRATCH/shots/shoot.mjs`), add `$SCRATCH/shots/addr.mjs`, which opens a page, runs steps and prints the address after each:

```js
// Opens a page, then runs steps, printing the address after each:
//   node addr.mjs <url> <out.png> <width> <height> <waitMs> [step ...]
// A step is click:X,Y | wheel:X,Y,DY | back | forward | wait:MS | shot:FILE
import { chromium } from "playwright";
const [, , url, out, width, height, waitMs, ...steps] = process.argv;
const browser = await chromium.launch({ executablePath: "/opt/pw-browsers/chromium-1194/chrome-linux/chrome", args: ["--use-gl=swiftshader", "--enable-webgl", "--ignore-gpu-blocklist"] });
const page = await browser.newPage({ viewport: { width: Number(width), height: Number(height) } });
const errors = [];
page.on("pageerror", (e) => errors.push(String(e).slice(0, 300)));
page.on("console", (m) => { if (m.type() === "error") errors.push(`console: ${m.text()}`.slice(0, 300)); });
await page.goto(url, { waitUntil: "load", timeout: 120000 });
await page.waitForTimeout(Number(waitMs));
const where = async (label) => console.log(`${label}: ${decodeURIComponent(new URL(page.url()).search) || "(none)"}`);
await where("loaded");
for (const s of steps) {
  const [kind, arg = ""] = s.split(/:(.*)/s);
  if (kind === "click") { const [x, y] = arg.split(",").map(Number); await page.mouse.click(x, y); await page.waitForTimeout(2500); }
  else if (kind === "wheel") { const [x, y, dy] = arg.split(",").map(Number); await page.mouse.move(x, y); for (let i = 0; i < 6; i++) { await page.mouse.wheel(0, dy / 6); await page.waitForTimeout(60); } await page.waitForTimeout(2500); }
  else if (kind === "back") { await page.goBack(); await page.waitForTimeout(3000); }
  else if (kind === "forward") { await page.goForward(); await page.waitForTimeout(3000); }
  else if (kind === "wait") { await page.waitForTimeout(Number(arg)); }
  else if (kind === "shot") { await page.screenshot({ path: arg }); }
  await where(s);
}
await page.screenshot({ path: out });
for (const e of errors) console.log("error", e);
await browser.close();
```

Run from `$SCRATCH/shots` (a 1440 × 900 window; the map spans x 256–1008 under a 64 px bar, the panel x 1008–1440):

Expected: Nairobi's wards shaded by doors with the 17 constituency names; the bar says "Nairobi County" with Map and People; the switches "Doors · Support · 2022 vote" and the hint "Click a ward to drop in, or a constituency's name" at the top left, the legend under them; the panel's five numbers two to a row, "Last time here", "Doors" (houses knocked, supporters among doors scored, doors this week, agents out today) and "Do this next" with Ruai ward first ("71,022 of 71,025 houses not knocked in 30 days", "Open the ward").
- `node addr.mjs "http://127.0.0.1:5198/voters?area=w:kileleshwa&house=6GCRPQM6%2BV9F4" house.png 1440 900 14000 click:657,402 back`. Expected: loaded at Kabarsiran Avenue, the house outlined in orange, houses coloured, the credits along the bottom in full and the address unchanged (`loaded: ?area=w:kileleshwa&house=6GCRPQM6+V9F4`); the click opens another house as a step (`house=6GCRPQM6+X95Q`); Back returns to `house=6GCRPQM6+V9F4`.
- `node addr.mjs "http://127.0.0.1:5198/voters?area=w:kileleshwa&house=6GCRPQM6%2BV9F4" out.png 1440 900 14000 wheel:632,482,3000 wheel:632,482,3000`. Expected: `?area=w:kileleshwa` (leaving street level closes the house), then `(none)` (the county).

### Task 12: House card and search

**Files:**
- Create: `src/components/gw/voters/HouseCard.tsx`
- Create: `src/components/gw/voters/MapSearch.tsx`
- Modify: `src/lib/house-source.ts` (`houseAt`), `src/lib/houses.ts` (`blockNote`), `src/lib/voters-map.ts` (`searchGo`, `PLACE_ZOOM`)
- Modify: `src/routes/_authenticated/voters.tsx`, `src/components/gw/voters/MapPanel.tsx` (a `card` slot), `src/styles/groundwork.css`
- Test: `tests/door-place.test.ts`, `tests/houses.test.ts`, `tests/voters-map.test.ts`
- Harness only (not committed): `$SCRATCH/harness/vite.config.ts` (a stand-in key) and `$SCRATCH/shots/addr.mjs` (search steps, MapTiler faked)

Changes from the design, found by running it:
- **The footprint comes from the house file** (`houseAt`: the tile the plus code's centre is in), not from the click, so a link or a search opens the card complete. The canvas's footprint argument to `onHouse` goes unused.
- **The card comes first in the panel**, above the area's numbers, rather than in place of them: on a phone the sheet shows the top of the panel (Task 13). Its button says "Close", since the browser's Back already steps out of a house. Leaving street level closing the house is Task 11's.
- **The search box lives in the bar, the camera in the map.** The box's picks reach the map through a ref (`pick`), which keeps Task 11's layout as it is. A place leaves its own camera (`pending`) for the flight its new area starts, or flies at once when the area stays the same.
- **Places need the MapTiler key.** Without it the box searches wards and people, and says so.

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `housesInTile` (Task 8); `toTileSpace` (Task 2); `plusCodeCentre`, `wardAt` (Task 2); `BLOCK_M2`, `OUTCOME_COLOUR`, `OUTCOME_LABEL`, `getHouse`, `HouseVisit` (Task 6); `searchHits`, `geocodeUrl`, `placeHits`, `SearchHit`, `CameraTarget` (Task 7); from Task 11 the screen's `MapScreen` (its camera, `fromMap`, `addressKey`), `MapPanel`, `Bar` (its `children` slot) and `nextSearch`, `SearchChanges`. Unchanged: `maskPhone` (`lib/phone.ts`), `band` and `BAND_COLOUR` (`lib/wardmap.ts`), `PersonRow`.
- Produces:
  - `houseAt(area: MapArea, pc: string, fetcher = fetch): Promise<Building | null>` in `house-source.ts`.
  - `blockNote(m2: number | null): string | null` in `houses.ts`.
  - `PLACE_ZOOM = 16.5` and `searchGo(hit: SearchHit, shapes: WardShape[]): { changes: SearchChanges; camera: CameraTarget | null }` in `voters-map.ts`.
  - `HouseCard({ pc, mapArea, people, onClose, onPerson? })` and `MapSearch({ mapArea, maptilerKey, wards, people, onPick })`; `MapPanel` takes `card?: ReactNode`.

How it works:
- **House card** (`?house=`, map tab): "House", the plus code and "Close"; "Footprint about N m²", and when the outline is `BLOCK_M2` or more, "This outline covers N m², often several homes: log each household as its own visit."; "On file here", the people whose `PersonRow.house` is this plus code, with masked phones and their support band, each opening their record on the People tab (agents get no list: they have no People data); "Visits" from `getHouse`, newest first: date, outcome, support, issue, the agent and the person. Before the migration the visits say "The house data isn't in yet."
- **Search** (map tab, in the bar): a combobox. Typing lists Wards, Constituencies (a county's map only), People (by name, or by number from four digits; not for agents) and Places (MapTiler, after a 300 ms pause, three letters or more). The arrow keys move, Enter picks, Escape closes; a click picks too. A ward or constituency becomes the area; a person with a house opens it in the ward it stands in; a person without one opens their record on the People tab; a place opens the ward there with the camera at zoom 16.5 on the place.

```diff
+/** The house card's note for an outline big enough to be several homes; null below BLOCK_M2. */
+export function blockNote(m2: number | null): string | null {
+  return m2 !== null && m2 >= BLOCK_M2

   const bytes = await archive.getTile(HOUSE_ZOOM, x, y);
   return bytes ? decodeTile(x, y, bytes) : [];
 }
+
+/** The house a plus code names, from the tile its centre is in; null when the file has none there. */
+export async function houseAt(
+  area: MapArea,
+  pc: string,
+  fetcher: Fetch = fetch,
+): Promise<Building | null> {
+  const code = pc.trim().toUpperCase();
+  const centre = plusCodeCentre(code);
+  if (!centre) return null;
+  const [x, y] = toTileSpace(centre);
+  const houses = await housesInTile(area, Math.floor(x), Math.floor(y), fetcher);
+  return houses.find((h) => h.id === code) ?? null;
+}

+/**
+ * What choosing a search hit does: a ward or constituency becomes the area on
+ * show; a person with a house opens it, in the ward it stands in; a person
+ * without one opens their record on the People tab; a place opens the ward
+ * there, with the camera on the place (outside every ward, the area stays).
+ */
+export function searchGo(
+  hit: SearchHit,
+  shapes: WardShape[],
+): { changes: SearchChanges; camera: CameraTarget | null } {
+  if (hit.kind === "ward")
+    return { changes: { area: `w:${hit.slug}`, house: undefined }, camera: null };
+  if (hit.kind === "constituency")
+    return { changes: { area: `c:${hit.name}`, house: undefined }, camera: null };
+  if (hit.kind === "person") {
+    if (!hit.house) return { changes: { tab: "people", person: hit.id }, camera: null };
+    const at = plusCodeCentre(hit.house);
+    const slug = (at ? wardAt(at, shapes)?.slug : undefined) ?? hit.wardSlug;
+    return { changes: { ...(slug ? { area: `w:${slug}` } : {}), house: hit.house }, camera: null };
+  }
+  const ward = wardAt(hit.center, shapes);
+  return {
+    changes: { ...(ward ? { area: `w:${ward.slug}` } : {}), house: undefined },
+    camera: { kind: "point", center: hit.center, zoom: PLACE_ZOOM },
+  };
+}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Write `src/components/gw/voters/HouseCard.tsx`**

```tsx
// A house on the Voters map: its plus code and footprint (from the house
// file, so a link opens it as well as a click), a note when the outline is
// big enough to be several homes, who is on file there, and its visits.

import { useQuery } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { useServerFn } from "@tanstack/react-start";

import type { PersonRow } from "@/lib/console.functions";
import { houseAt } from "@/lib/house-source";
import { blockNote, OUTCOME_COLOUR, OUTCOME_LABEL } from "@/lib/houses";
import { getHouse } from "@/lib/houses.functions";
import type { MapArea } from "@/lib/map-areas";
import { maskPhone } from "@/lib/phone";
import { band, BAND_COLOUR } from "@/lib/wardmap";

const nf = new Intl.NumberFormat("en-KE");
const day = (iso: string) =>
  new Date(iso).toLocaleDateString("en-GB", { day: "2-digit", month: "short" });

export function HouseCard({
  pc,
  mapArea,
  people,
  onClose,
  onPerson,
}: {
  pc: string;
  mapArea: MapArea;
  /** Everyone on file; null for agents, who don't get the People list. */
  people: PersonRow[] | null;
  onClose: () => void;
  /** Opens a record on the People tab; left out for agents. */
  onPerson?: (id: string) => void;
}) {
  const fetchHouse = useServerFn(getHouse);
  const { data: house, isPending: outlinePending } = useQuery({
    queryKey: ["house-at", mapArea.id, pc],
    queryFn: () => houseAt(mapArea, pc),
    staleTime: Infinity,
  });
  const { data: visits } = useQuery({
    queryKey: ["house", pc],
    queryFn: () => fetchHouse({ data: { pc } }),
  });
  const here = (people ?? []).filter((p) => p.house === pc);
  const note = blockNote(house?.area ?? null);

  return (
    <section className="vhouse" aria-label={`House ${pc}`}>
      <div className="card-head">
        <div>
          <span className="eyebrow">House</span>
          <h2 className="mono">{pc}</h2>
        </div>
        <button type="button" className="btn btn--ghost btn--sm" onClick={onClose}>
          Close
        </button>
      </div>
      <p className="meta">
        {house
          ? `Footprint about ${nf.format(house.area)} m²`
          : outlinePending
            ? "Reading its outline…"
            : "Its outline isn't in the house file."}
      </p>
      {note ? <p className="vhouse-note">{note}</p> : null}

      {people ? (
        <>
          <h3>On file here</h3>
          {here.length ? (
            <div className="f-rows">
              {here.map((p) => {
                const b = band(p.support);
                return (
                  <div className="f-row" key={p.id}>
                    <span>
                      {onPerson ? (
                        <button type="button" className="vt-link" onClick={() => onPerson(p.id)}>
                          {p.name}
                        </button>
                      ) : (
                        <b>{p.name}</b>
                      )}{" "}
                      <span className="mono dim">{maskPhone(p.phone)}</span>
                    </span>
                    <span>
                      {b ? (
                        <>
                          <i className="vdot" style={{ background: BAND_COLOUR[b] }} />
                          Support {b} of 5
                        </>
                      ) : (
                        "Not scored"
                      )}
                    </span>
                  </div>
                );
              })}
            </div>
          ) : (
            <p className="meta">No one on file is placed at this house yet.</p>
          )}
        </>
      ) : null}

      <h3>Visits</h3>
      {visits === undefined ? (
        <p className="meta">Reading the visits…</p>
      ) : visits === null ? (
        <p className="meta">The house data isn't in yet.</p>
      ) : visits.length ? (
        <ol className="vvisits">
          {visits.map((v, i) => (
            <li key={`${v.at}-${i}`}>
              <span className="mono">{day(v.at)}</span>
              <span>
                <b style={{ color: OUTCOME_COLOUR[v.outcome] }}>{OUTCOME_LABEL[v.outcome]}</b>
                {v.support ? ` · support ${v.support} of 5` : ""}
                {v.issue ? ` · ${v.issue}` : ""}
              </span>
              <small>
                {v.agent ?? "Agent not on record"}
                {v.person ? ` · ${v.person.name}` : ""}
              </small>
            </li>
          ))}
        </ol>
      ) : (
        <p className="meta">No visits recorded at this house yet.</p>
      )}
    </section>
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Write `src/components/gw/voters/MapSearch.tsx`**

```tsx
// The one search box above the Voters map: wards and constituencies by name,
// people on file by name or number, and places and streets from MapTiler when
// the key is set. A combobox: the arrow keys move through the results, Enter
// picks one, Escape closes them.

import { useQuery } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { useEffect, useId, useMemo, useRef, useState, type KeyboardEvent } from "react";

import type { MapArea } from "@/lib/map-areas";
import { geocodeUrl, placeHits, searchHits, type SearchHit } from "@/lib/voters-map";

const GROUPS: { kind: SearchHit["kind"]; label: string }[] = [
  { kind: "ward", label: "Wards" },
  { kind: "constituency", label: "Constituencies" },
  { kind: "person", label: "People" },
  { kind: "place", label: "Places" },
];

export function MapSearch({
  mapArea,
  maptilerKey,
  wards,
  people,
  onPick,
}: {
  mapArea: MapArea;
  /** Places and streets come from MapTiler's search with the map's key; none without it. */
  maptilerKey: string | null;
  wards: { slug: string; name: string; constituency: string }[];
  /** People on file; none for agents, who don't search the records. */
  people: { id: string; name: string; phone: string; ward: string | null; house: string | null }[];
  onPick: (hit: SearchHit) => void;
}) {
  const id = useId();
  const input = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);
  const [q, setQ] = useState("");
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
  const [active, setActive] = useState(0);
  // Places are looked up once typing pauses.
  const [settled, setSettled] = useState("");
  useEffect(() => {
    const t = setTimeout(() => setSettled(q), 300);
    return () => clearTimeout(t);
  }, [q]);
  const url = geocodeUrl(settled, maptilerKey, mapArea.bounds);
  const { data: places = [], isFetching } = useQuery({
    queryKey: ["geocode", url],
    queryFn: async () => {
      const res = await fetch(url!);
      return res.ok ? placeHits(await res.json()) : [];
    },
    enabled: Boolean(url),
    staleTime: 5 * 60_000,
  });
  const hits = useMemo(
    () =>
      q.trim()
        ? [...searchHits(q, wards, people, mapArea.top), ...(settled === q ? places : [])]
        : [],
    [q, settled, wards, people, mapArea.top, places],
  );
  const shown = open && hits.length > 0;
  const nothing = open && q.trim().length >= 2 && !hits.length && settled === q && !isFetching;

  function pick(hit: SearchHit) {
    onPick(hit);
    setQ("");
    setOpen(false);
    input.current?.blur();
  }

  function onKeyDown(e: KeyboardEvent<HTMLInputElement>) {
    if (e.key === "ArrowDown" || e.key === "ArrowUp") {
      e.preventDefault();
      setOpen(true);
      const n = hits.length;
      if (n) setActive((a) => (e.key === "ArrowDown" ? (a + 1) % n : (a - 1 + n) % n));
    } else if (e.key === "Enter") {
      const hit = hits[active];
      if (shown && hit) {
        e.preventDefault();
        pick(hit);
      }
    } else if (e.key === "Escape") {
      setOpen(false);
    }
  }

  return (
    <div className="vsearch">
      <input
        ref={input}
        type="search"
        role="combobox"
        aria-label="Search wards, people and places"
        aria-autocomplete="list"
        aria-expanded={shown}
        aria-controls={`${id}-list`}
        {...(shown && hits[active] ? { "aria-activedescendant": `${id}-${active}` } : {})}
        placeholder={maptilerKey ? "Search wards, people, places" : "Search wards and people"}
        value={q}
        onChange={(e) => {
          setQ(e.target.value);
          setActive(0);
          setOpen(true);
        }}
        onFocus={() => setOpen(true)}
        onBlur={() => setOpen(false)}
        onKeyDown={onKeyDown}
      />
      {shown ? (
        <ul id={`${id}-list`} role="listbox" className="vsearch-list" aria-label="Results">
          {GROUPS.map((g) => {
            const inGroup = hits.flatMap((h, i) => (h.kind === g.kind ? [{ h, i }] : []));
            if (!inGroup.length) return null;
            return [
              <li key={g.kind} role="presentation" className="vsearch-group">
                {g.label}
              </li>,
              ...inGroup.map(({ h, i }) => (
                <li
                  key={`${g.kind}-${i}`}
                  id={`${id}-${i}`}
                  role="option"
                  aria-selected={i === active}
                  className="vsearch-opt"
                  // Keep the focus in the box, so the pick lands before it closes.
                  onMouseDown={(e) => e.preventDefault()}
                  onMouseEnter={() => setActive(i)}
                  onClick={() => pick(h)}
                >
                  <span>{h.label}</span>
                  <small>{h.sub}</small>
                </li>
              )),
            ];
          })}
        </ul>
      ) : nothing ? (
        <p className="vsearch-list vsearch-none" role="status">
          Nothing by that name in this area.
        </p>
      ) : null}
    </div>
  );
}
```

Replace `$SCRATCH/shots/addr.mjs` with this version, which can type into the search box and fake MapTiler (`MAPTILER=fake`: its styles answered with OpenFreeMap's, its search with one place):

```js
// Opens a page, then runs steps, printing the address after each:
//   node addr.mjs <url> <out.png> <width> <height> <waitMs> [step ...]
// A step is click:X,Y | wheel:X,Y,DY | back | forward | wait:MS | shot:FILE
//   | type:TEXT (into the search box) | key:KEY | options (prints the search's options)
import { chromium } from "playwright";
const [, , url, out, width, height, waitMs, ...steps] = process.argv;
const browser = await chromium.launch({ executablePath: "/opt/pw-browsers/chromium-1194/chrome-linux/chrome", args: ["--use-gl=swiftshader", "--enable-webgl", "--ignore-gpu-blocklist"] });
const page = await browser.newPage({ viewport: { width: Number(width), height: Number(height) } });
const errors = [];
// MAPTILER=fake: answer MapTiler's requests here (the harness then runs with a stand-in key):
// its styles with OpenFreeMap's, its search with one place, Ngong Road in Kilimani.
if (process.env.MAPTILER === "fake") {
  await page.route("https://api.maptiler.com/**", async (route) => {
    const u = new URL(route.request().url());
    if (u.pathname.endsWith("/style.json")) return route.continue({ url: "https://tiles.openfreemap.org/styles/liberty" });
    if (u.pathname.startsWith("/geocoding/")) return route.fulfill({ json: { features: [{ text: "Ngong Road", place_name: "Ngong Road, Kilimani, Nairobi", center: [36.787, -1.2921] }] } });
    return route.fulfill({ contentType: "image/svg+xml", body: '<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="67" height="20"/>' });
  });
}
page.on("pageerror", (e) => errors.push(String(e).slice(0, 300)));
page.on("console", (m) => { if (m.type() === "error") errors.push(`console: ${m.text()}`.slice(0, 300)); });
await page.goto(url, { waitUntil: "load", timeout: 120000 });
await page.waitForTimeout(Number(waitMs));
const where = async (label) => console.log(`${label}: ${decodeURIComponent(new URL(page.url()).search) || "(none)"}`);
await where("loaded");
for (const s of steps) {
  const [kind, arg = ""] = s.split(/:(.*)/s);
  if (kind === "click") { const [x, y] = arg.split(",").map(Number); await page.mouse.click(x, y); await page.waitForTimeout(2500); }
  else if (kind === "wheel") { const [x, y, dy] = arg.split(",").map(Number); await page.mouse.move(x, y); for (let i = 0; i < 6; i++) { await page.mouse.wheel(0, dy / 6); await page.waitForTimeout(60); } await page.waitForTimeout(2500); }
  else if (kind === "back") { await page.goBack(); await page.waitForTimeout(3000); }
  else if (kind === "forward") { await page.goForward(); await page.waitForTimeout(3000); }
  else if (kind === "wait") { await page.waitForTimeout(Number(arg)); }
  else if (kind === "shot") { await page.screenshot({ path: arg }); }
  else if (kind === "type") { const box = page.getByRole("combobox"); await box.click(); await box.fill(""); await box.type(arg, { delay: 40 }); await page.waitForTimeout(900); }
  else if (kind === "key") { await page.keyboard.press(arg); await page.waitForTimeout(2500); }
  else if (kind === "options") { console.log(`options: ${(await page.getByRole("option").allTextContents()).join(" | ")}`); continue; }
  await where(s);
}
await page.screenshot({ path: out });
for (const e of errors) console.log("error", e);
await browser.close();
```

Expected: the card at the top of the panel ("House", `6GCRPQM6+V9F4`, "Footprint about 106 m²", on file "Wanjiru Kamau +254 710 ••• 000 · Support 4 of 5", one visit "06 Sept Spoke · support 1 of 5 · Roads" by Harness Manager; the dates move with the day the harness runs); Close gives `?area=w:kileleshwa` with the map left where it was; Back reopens the house; the name gives `…&tab=people…&person=00000000-0000-4000-a000-000000000001`, the People tab with her record.

### Task 13: The bottom sheet

**Files:**
- Create: `src/lib/sheet.ts` (the sheet's heights, pure)
- Create: `src/components/gw/voters/Sheet.tsx`
- Modify: `src/routes/_authenticated/voters.tsx` (the panel goes in a `Sheet`; the map's padding), `src/components/gw/voters/VotersCanvas.tsx` (two fixes, below), `src/styles/groundwork.css`
- Test: `tests/sheet.test.ts`
- Harness only (not committed): four more steps in `$SCRATCH/shots/addr.mjs`

Changes from the design, found by running it at phone width:
- **The sheet follows what's on show.** An opened house raises a peeking sheet to half, so its card shows; a new area lowers a full sheet to half, so the flight to it shows. The map's zoom buttons, credits and MapTiler logo move up above the sheet (`--vsheet`, the room it takes), so the credits stay visible.
- **Two fixes to `VotersCanvas`.** (1) MapLibre's `resize` ends with a `moveend` of its own. On a phone, opening a ward adds a line of crumbs to the bar, the map gets shorter, and that `moveend` was taken as the person's move: the address went back to the county. A `moveend` right after a resize is now ignored. (2) Changing the padding jumps the camera, which stopped the flight that had just started (the sheet's room changes by a few pixels when the bar grows). Flights now carry the padding, and a new padding waits until the camera stops.
- The search box shrinks to 160 px before wrapping, so on a phone it shares a line with the Map/People tabs.

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: from Task 11 the screen's `MapScreen` and its `padding` prop to `VotersCanvas`; from Task 10 `VotersCanvas`'s `fly`, `moveend` handler and padding effect.
- Produces:
  - In `src/lib/sheet.ts`: `type Snap = "peek" | "half" | "full"`, `PEEK_PX = 132`, `snapHeight(snap, body): number`, `nearestSnap(height, body): Snap`, `nextSnap(snap): Snap`, `mapRoom(snap, body): number` (at most half the map).
  - `Sheet({ label, raiseOn?, lowerOn?, onRoom?, children })`: wider than 1,100 px, the plain `.vpanel` aside; narrower, the sheet, reporting through `onRoom` the room it takes from the map.

How it works: below 1,100 px the `.vbody` is one cell, the map, and the sheet lies over its bottom (`position: absolute`). It has three heights: peek (132 px, or the map's height if less), half (half the map, never below peek) and full (the map's height). The handle is a button: a press steps peek → half → full → peek (its label says what the next press does), and a drag (pointer events, captured) follows the finger and snaps to the nearest height on release. The map's `padding.bottom` is the sheet's room, at most half the map, so the camera centres on what is still visible. Under `prefers-reduced-motion` the sheet doesn't animate.

- [ ] **Step 3: Write `src/lib/sheet.ts`**

```ts
// The Voters panel as a sheet on a narrow screen: its three heights, the one
// a drag lets go nearest, the next one its handle steps to, and the room it
// takes from the map. Pure.

export type Snap = "peek" | "half" | "full";

/** How much shows when the sheet peeks: the top of a house card, or the first numbers. */
export const PEEK_PX = 132;

const SNAPS: Snap[] = ["peek", "half", "full"];

/** The sheet's height at a snap, over a map `body` pixels tall. */
export function snapHeight(snap: Snap, body: number): number {
  const peek = Math.min(PEEK_PX, body);
  if (snap === "peek") return peek;
  if (snap === "half") return Math.max(Math.round(body / 2), peek);
  return body;
}

/** The snap nearest a height the sheet was dragged to. */
export function nearestSnap(height: number, body: number): Snap {
  let best: Snap = "peek";
  for (const s of SNAPS)
    if (Math.abs(snapHeight(s, body) - height) < Math.abs(snapHeight(best, body) - height))
      best = s;
  return best;
}

/** Where the handle, pressed, takes the sheet: up a step, and from full back down to peek. */
export const nextSnap = (snap: Snap): Snap =>
  snap === "peek" ? "half" : snap === "half" ? "full" : "peek";

/** The room the sheet takes from the bottom of the map: at most half, so the map keeps a centre. */
export const mapRoom = (snap: Snap, body: number): number =>
  Math.min(snapHeight(snap, body), Math.round(body / 2));
```

Run: `npx -y tsx --tsconfig tsconfig.json tests/sheet.test.ts`

- [ ] **Step 4: Write `src/components/gw/voters/Sheet.tsx`**

```tsx
// The Voters panel. Wider than 1,100 pixels it sits beside the map; narrower,
// it is a sheet over the bottom of the map that drags between three heights
// (peek, half and the map's full height) and snaps to the nearest when let
// go. Its handle is also a button that steps up through them. An opened house
// raises a peeking sheet to half; a new area lowers a full one, so the flight
// shows.

import { useEffect, useRef, useState, type PointerEvent, type ReactNode } from "react";

import { mapRoom, nearestSnap, nextSnap, snapHeight, type Snap } from "@/lib/sheet";

const NARROW = "(max-width: 1100px)";
const HANDLE: Record<Snap, string> = {
  peek: "Show more of the panel",
  half: "Show the whole panel",
  full: "Show the map",
};

/** Whether the screen is narrow enough for the sheet; false until mounted, as on the server. */
function useNarrow(): boolean {
  const [narrow, setNarrow] = useState(false);
  useEffect(() => {
    const m = window.matchMedia(NARROW);
    const on = () => setNarrow(m.matches);
    on();
    m.addEventListener("change", on);
    return () => m.removeEventListener("change", on);
  }, []);
  return narrow;
}

export function Sheet({
  label,
  raiseOn = null,
  lowerOn = null,
  onRoom,
  children,
}: {
  label: string;
  /** A new value here (an opened house) raises a peeking sheet to half. */
  raiseOn?: string | null;
  /** A new value here (a new area) lowers a full sheet to half. */
  lowerOn?: string | null;
  /** The room the sheet takes from the bottom of the map, for its padding; 0 when wide. */
  onRoom?: (px: number) => void;
  children: ReactNode;
}) {
  const narrow = useNarrow();
  const box = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const [body, setBody] = useState(0);
  const [snap, setSnap] = useState<Snap>("peek");
  const [drag, setDrag] = useState<number | null>(null);
  const start = useRef<{ y: number; h: number } | null>(null);
  const dragged = useRef(false);

  // The map's height, which the three snaps are measured against.
  useEffect(() => {
    const parent = box.current?.parentElement;
    if (!narrow || !parent) return;
    const measure = () => setBody(parent.clientHeight);
    measure();
    const ro = new ResizeObserver(measure);
    ro.observe(parent);
    return () => ro.disconnect();
  }, [narrow]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (raiseOn) setSnap((s) => (s === "peek" ? "half" : s));
  }, [raiseOn]);
  const lastLower = useRef(lowerOn);
  useEffect(() => {
    if (lastLower.current === lowerOn) return;
    lastLower.current = lowerOn;
    setSnap((s) => (s === "full" ? "half" : s));
  }, [lowerOn]);

  const room = narrow ? mapRoom(snap, body) : 0;
  useEffect(() => {
    onRoom?.(room);
  }, [room, onRoom]);

  if (!narrow)
    return (
      <aside className="vpanel" aria-label={label}>
        {children}
      </aside>
    );

  function down(e: PointerEvent<HTMLButtonElement>) {
    e.currentTarget.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId);
    start.current = { y: e.clientY, h: snapHeight(snap, body) };
    dragged.current = false;
  }
  function move(e: PointerEvent<HTMLButtonElement>) {
    const s = start.current;
    if (!s) return;
    const dy = s.y - e.clientY;
    if (Math.abs(dy) > 4) dragged.current = true;
    if (dragged.current) setDrag(Math.min(Math.max(s.h + dy, snapHeight("peek", body)), body));
  }
  function up() {
    start.current = null;
    if (dragged.current && drag !== null) setSnap(nearestSnap(drag, body));
    setDrag(null);
  }

  return (
    <div
      ref={box}
      className={drag === null ? "vsheet" : "vsheet is-drag"}
      style={{ height: drag ?? snapHeight(snap, body) }}
    >
      <button
        type="button"
        className="vsheet-handle"
        aria-label={HANDLE[snap]}
        onPointerDown={down}
        onPointerMove={move}
        onPointerUp={up}
        onPointerCancel={up}
        onClick={() => {
          // A drag ends in a click too; only a press steps.
          if (dragged.current) dragged.current = false;
          else setSnap(nextSnap(snap));
        }}
      >
        <span aria-hidden="true" />
      </button>
      <aside className="vpanel" aria-label={label}>
        {children}
      </aside>
    </div>
  );
}
```

```js
  else if (kind === "handle") { await page.locator(".vsheet-handle").click(); await page.waitForTimeout(1500); }
```

**Files:**

- Modify: `src/lib/houses.ts` (takes `Band`, `band` and `BAND_COLOUR` from `wardmap.ts`), `src/components/gw/voters/HouseCard.tsx` and `tests/houses.test.ts` (their imports), `src/lib/map.functions.ts` (`getWardMap` goes), `src/lib/geo-files.ts` (only `getJson` stays), `src/lib/geo.ts` (`BuildingCollection` and `toBuildings` go), `src/lib/house-build.ts` (a comment), `AGENTS.md` (three lines)

Changes from the design:
- `VotersMap.tsx`, `WardMap.tsx`, `DoorsView.tsx` and `WardsView.tsx` already went in Task 11.
- **`geo-files.ts` stays, with only `getJson`:** the elections map (`elections/AreaMap.tsx`), the demo map (`demo/ChoroplethMap.tsx`) and the Voters screen fetch their JSON with it. The building-outline queries go.
- **`wardmap.ts` goes as well.** With `getWardMap` gone, only its support bands are used (the Support colours and the house card), so they move to `houses.ts`; the rest (`aggregate`, `summarise`, `STATUS`, the issue colours) was the old ward map's.
- The database's `ward_map` function stays: nothing calls it, and dropping it would need a migration of its own.

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: the house files, map areas and `house_visits` from Tasks 1–6; the screen from Tasks 10–13.
- Produces: `Band`, `band(score)` and `BAND_COLOUR` exported from `src/lib/houses.ts` (no longer from `src/lib/wardmap.ts`). Nothing else changes for callers.

```diff
+export type Band = 0 | 1 | 2 | 3 | 4 | 5;
+
+/** The 0-100 score back on the 1-5 door scale; 0 when unknown. The inverse of SUPPORT_SCORE. */
+export function band(score: number): Band {
+  if (!score || score <= 0) return 0;
+  if (score >= 85) return 5;
+  if (score >= 62) return 4;
+  if (score >= 40) return 3;
+  if (score >= 20) return 2;
+  return 1;
+}
+
+export const BAND_COLOUR: Record<Exclude<Band, 0>, string> = {
+  1: "hsl(0, 80%, 55%)",
+  2: "hsl(22, 90%, 57%)",
+  3: "hsl(45, 95%, 56%)",
+  4: "hsl(95, 60%, 48%)",

 - Inbox filters are grouped by queue, channel/sentiment and topic; message metadata is a separate block, with a two-pane tablet layout and stacked phone layout. Why: filters stay scannable and timestamps never run into message text.
 - The voters map uses MapLibre's native pan, wheel/pinch and bounded animated zoom; ward/building actions stay explained in-map. Why: field users need predictable spatial controls and one clear next step.
+- Houses come from one PMTiles file per map area in `public/tiles/` (Google Open Buildings v3, zoom-16 tiles, layer `buildings`, plus code `pc`), built by `scripts/geo/house-tiles.ts`, each under 25 MiB; a campaign's area is `campaigns.map_area`, and none means no map. Why: every house in the area, read a tile at a time by the map and the field app, from the app's own host.
+- A door at a house is a `house_visits` row, written only by `record_door`; the map's colours read it through `house_doors()` and `ward_houses()`, and every read fails soft before the migration. Why: one retry-safe record per door, scoped to the campaign by row-level security.
+- The Voters map's level follows the zoom (area 8–11, constituency 11–13, ward 13–16, street 16–20), and its address holds the area, house, colour, base map and tab: the person's moves write it, the app's flights don't. Why: zooming in and clicking in land in the same place, and a link, a reload or Back reopens the same view.
 - Morning briefings reserve an atomic database lease, save dated no-source outcomes, and retry only missing or zero-source automated rows on later hourly runs; provider blocks stay paused. Why: daily checks remain visible without fabricating news or duplicating paid work.
 - The hourly callback catches collection failures and checks morning records before rival work. Why: a failed search must not skip the daily briefing check.
 - The shared archive reads monthly morning stories and diary through authenticated user-scoped clients. Why: historical campaign records retain the same isolation as current records.
```

- [ ] **Step 7: Release, with the user**

1. **Ask the user before pushing anything.** Show them the commits since the branch left `main` (`git log --oneline main..HEAD`) and the screenshots from Tasks 10–13.
2. **Schema number.** See which number `main` is at:
   ```bash
   git fetch origin main
   for f in $(git ls-tree --name-only origin/main supabase/migrations/ | sort -r); do
     if git show "origin/main:$f" | grep -q "function public.groundwork_schema_version"; then
       echo "$f"; git show "origin/main:$f" | grep -A4 "function public.groundwork_schema_version" | grep "select"; break
     fi
   done
   ```
   On 2026-10-12 this printed `supabase/migrations/20261007090000_election_atlas.sql` and `as $$ select 22 $$;`. If `main` already returns 23 (the area-profiles plan got there first), rename `supabase/migrations/20261012090000_voters_map.sql` to a timestamp after `main`'s newest migration, make its `groundwork_schema_version()` return 24, change `assert public.groundwork_schema_version() >= 23` in `tests/sql/houses.test.sql` to 24, rerun the SQL tests, and commit.
3. **With their OK, push the branch** (`git push -u origin <branch>`). Merging into `main` is the user's step (or a pull request if they ask for one): `main` syncs to Lovable, which publishes the code before the migration is applied, and every new read fails soft until then (Review Focus 1).
4. **The migration.** Ask the user to have Lovable apply `20261012090000_voters_map.sql` (in Lovable's chat). Then, in Lovable's SQL runner or Supabase's SQL editor (the user runs these; never ask for credentials):
   ```sql
   select public.groundwork_schema_version();                       -- 23
   select slug, map_area from public.campaigns order by slug;       -- sakaja and kalonzo: nairobi; mathira: nyeri/mathira
   select to_regclass('public.house_visits');                       -- house_visits
   select count(*) from public.house_visits;                        -- the door events with a house, copied in once
   ```
5. **On the live site,** with the user: Voters opens on the campaign's map; Kileleshwa at street level shows its houses; the field app's "Which house am I at?" finds a house outside Kileleshwa.
